# v2.4 Money First, Nothing Manual — Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A builder's first EarnKit answer leads with money they can win (EarnKit's own campaigns first, then what their repos already qualify for), entering a campaign takes one confirmation, and scoring, freezing, review and payout run on their own from a free daily cron.

**Architecture:**
- **Identity and data:** one installation per OAuth client, and every wallet an installation used. New tables hold counted wallets, the campaign lifecycle, token fees and wallets. Everything is in one idempotent `db/schema.sql` block.
- **Money in the program file:** EarnKit campaigns carry a USD prize. Their reward, summary and rules are generated from the fields, and `pnpm campaign golive` prices MINI on DefiLlama and writes the file.
- **Scoring v2:** direct senders plus internal-call callers, the new history rules and exclusions. Counted wallets are stored, and each entry's errors are isolated.
- **Entry v2:** proof by deployer signature, every problem returned at once, the daily limit charged only for recorded entries, and a prize-first confirmation table.
- **List and alerts:** `list_programs` is a table renderer. An alerts engine opens every tool reply with "Since you last checked".
- **Lifecycle:** `POST /api/cron` (GitHub Actions, hourly) scores, freezes, opens a 48 h public review with a secret veto link, and pays from a capped hot wallet. Each payout is signed and stored before it is broadcast, so it can't be sent twice.
- **Outside this repo:** a Base campaign starter in `earnkitai/chain-kits` carries EarnKit's Builder Code (ERC-8021).

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16 (app router), postgres.js, zod v4, viem 2.57 (+ `ox/erc8021`), node:test via `tsx --test`, Foundry/anvil, GitHub Actions.

**Spec:** `docs/design-docs-and-implementation-plans/v2.4-money-first.md` (approved, binding). The UX audit it fixes: `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jenil/b99874fd-aee4-4d37-a607-aa6795dfc270/scratchpad/ux-map.md` (items C1–C24).

## Global Constraints

- **Where:** worktree `/Users/jenil/earnkit-v24`, branch `v2.4-money-first` (based on `main` d924e4a, live on earnkit.com). Commit after every task. Never push, merge, deploy, or set Railway or GitHub secrets: the owner does that.
- **First, once:** `cd /Users/jenil/earnkit-v24 && pnpm install` (the worktree has no `node_modules`).
- **Builder-facing text:** short, money first, markdown tables, no emojis (owner preference). Every prize shows a USD value where one is known. When no value is known, the organizer's words are shown as stated (e.g. "milestone-based USDC grants"); never invent one. One closing offer, never a menu. `show_to_builder` is always "show exactly as written".
- **Tokens are never advertised.** The only token text a builder sees is the one default row in the submission confirmation (`$TIP on tokens.fun, you keep 40% of fees (say "no token" to skip)`), with the terms footnote under the table. No "Entries get a token" anywhere: not in listings, `get_program`, campaign pages, or tool descriptions beyond what an agent needs to pass it. `token_policy` stays a funder setting, never displayed. v2.3's consent and ownership rules are unchanged: `builder_confirmed: true` plus an explicit `token.symbol`, the gate, the `token: <SYMBOL> <code>` HMAC line, one token per repo, and 3 launches a day.
- **Token fee alerts** only at $100 or more (`FEE_ALERT_USD = 100`; the spec says "above $100", and the brief says "≥ $100": ≥ is used). Fees are read with `availableFees(creator_wallet, WETH)` on Clanker's Base FeeLocker `0xf3622742b1e446d92e45e22923ef11c2fcd55d68`, where WETH is `0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006`. It is a free RPC read, cached 6 h, and counts only fees already in the locker.
- **Cost:** no new paid services. The scheduler is a GitHub Actions cron that sends `POST https://earnkit.com/api/cron` with `Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET`. Prices come from DefiLlama's free API (`https://coins.llama.fi/prices/current/base:<addr>`). Chain data comes from the public Base RPC and Base Blockscout.
- **Keys:**
  - `CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY` is the only key on Railway. The owner sets it with `railway variable set CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY --stdin`. Its wallet holds one campaign's prize plus gas.
  - Operator, guardian and deployer keys never go on Railway.
  - The old local `PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY` path (`pnpm campaign payout --execute`) is removed.
- **New env:**
  - `CRON_SECRET` (32+ characters; on Railway and as a GitHub Actions secret).
  - `ADMIN_VETO_SECRET` (32+ characters; on Railway and in the owner's local shell).
  - `EARNKIT_BUILDER_CODE` (optional, default `earnkit`).
  - Test only: `EARNKIT_BLOCKSCOUT_URL`, honored only when `NODE_ENV` is `development` or `test`.
- **Campaign #1:**
  - $500 total, $100 × 5 winners, paid in MINI `0x534b7aAD1Cdb6F02eC48CAbe428f0D9131E40B07` (18 decimals, Base).
  - `amount_each` is fixed at go-live from DefiLlama, rounded up to 3 significant digits.
  - It runs 14 days from go-live.
  - It stays `status: closed` on this branch. The owner has it on hold, and `pnpm campaign golive earnkit-campaign-1-base --write` flips it at launch.
- **Lifecycle constants:**
  - `FINAL_GRACE_MS` = 15 min (exists).
  - `REVIEW_MS` = 48 h.
  - Cron budget: 4 min per call.
  - The workflow runs hourly. The endpoint rescores an active campaign only when its scores are over 20 h old (over 1 h old in the last day), so the effect is daily scoring plus hourly at the end.
- **Builder Code:**
  - `EARNKIT_BUILDER_CODE` defaults to `"earnkit"`. The owner confirms the exact string after registering it at base.dev.
  - The ERC-8021 suffix lives in the new starter `earnkitai/chain-kits/base/campaign`. That work happens in a separate worktree of `/Users/jenil/earnkit-starters/chain-kits` from `origin/main` (Task 10).
- **Don't break production:**
  - Sponsor campaigns stay dormant without `ESCROW_*` (the 5 builder tools only).
  - Sponsor scoring uses scoring v2 too; it is a guide only.
  - `db/schema.sql` runs on every deploy (`pnpm predeploy`). Every statement must be idempotent and safe on existing rows: `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` with safe defaults, `CREATE … IF NOT EXISTS`, and the drop-then-add pattern for constraints. `pnpm db:check` (Task 1) proves it against main's schema with rows.
- **Never launch real tokens or send real funds in tests.**
  - Unit tests use fakes.
  - `pnpm campaign:e2e` uses a local Postgres (port 55442, data dir in the scratchpad), anvil `--chain-id 8453` on 127.0.0.1:8549 (it refuses anything that isn't anvil on 127.0.0.1), a mock MINI, a mock Blockscout, a mock raw GitHub and a mock tokens.fun.
  - The fork check runs on a local anvil fork (port 8550). It only reads the real network.
- **Code style:**
  - Match the surrounding code.
  - Exported functions get short doc comments ("Pure; exported for tests." where true).
  - Errors are one sentence naming the field and the fix.
  - Builder text never repeats another builder's text except as data (`quoted()`, `cleanText`).
- **Commands (from `/Users/jenil/earnkit-v24`):**
  - all unit tests: `pnpm -s test`;
  - one file: `npx tsx --test apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/<file>.test.ts`;
  - typecheck: `cd apps/frontend && npx tsc --noEmit -p .`;
  - build: `pnpm -s build`;
  - contracts: `cd contracts && forge build`.

## Review Focus

1. **Two different people using the same client app.** Cursor registers with a fixed `cursor://…` redirect URI. Today, client ids are a deterministic signature of the redirect URIs, so "one installation per client" would merge every Cursor user into one installation, with shared entries, payout wallets and wins. Expected: every registration is its own client (a random nonce in the client id), and two registrations never share an installation. Test in Task 1.
2. **Signed entry message spelling.** An agent may sign `EarnKit entry: https://github.com/Alice/TipJar 0xAbC…` (link form, checksummed wallet) instead of the normalized `github.com/alice/tipjar 0xabc…`. Expected: the entry is refused, and the problem quotes the exact text to sign. Test in Task 4.
3. **A crash between "transfer sent" and "prize_tx saved"**, or two cron runs at once. Expected: the signed transfer is stored (`sending`, its hash and raw bytes) before it is broadcast. The next run rebroadcasts the same bytes, so the same transaction is mined once, and it never signs a second transfer. Test in Task 7.
4. **Link-preview bots opening the veto link** (chat apps, mail scanners). Expected: GET only shows the review. Only a POST with the key and an entry id removes an entry, and only during the review. Test in Task 8.
5. **A table cell containing `|`** (a title or a repo note). Expected: escaped (`\|`) so the table renders; sponsor titles stay quoted. Test in Task 5.

```ts
const codeOf = (clientId: string) =>
  codeFor(clientId).searchParams.get('code')!;

test('v2.4 Review Focus 1: each registration is its own client, even with the same redirect URIs', () => {
  // Cursor-style apps register with one fixed redirect URI for every user.
  const a = registerClient({ redirect_uris: [redirect] }).client_id;
  const b = registerClient({ redirect_uris: [redirect] }).client_id;
  assert.notEqual(a, b);
  assert.notEqual(clientKeyOf(a), clientKeyOf(b));
});

test('v2.4: re-authorizing the same client keeps its client key (one installation); the token is new', () => {
  const { client_id } = registerClient({ redirect_uris: [redirect] });
  const first = exchangeCode(tokenForm(client_id, codeOf(client_id)));
  const second = exchangeCode(tokenForm(client_id, codeOf(client_id)));
  assert.notEqual(first.token, second.token);
  assert.equal(first.clientKey, second.clientKey);
  assert.equal(first.clientKey, clientKeyOf(client_id));
  assert.match(first.clientKey, /^[0-9a-f]{32}$/);
});

test('v2.4: a client id issued before v2.4 (no nonce) still authorizes', () => {
  const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ r: [redirect] })).toString('base64url');
  const sig = createHmac('sha256', 'test-secret').update(`client:${body}`).digest('base64url');
  const legacy = `${body}.${sig}`;
  const { token, clientKey } = exchangeCode(tokenForm(legacy, codeOf(legacy)));
  assert.match(token, /^ek_/);
  assert.equal(clientKey, clientKeyOf(legacy));
});
```

```ts
  const h = harness();
  h.deps.store!.rememberedWallet = async () => null;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Schema.** Append to `apps/frontend/db/schema.sql`:

```sql
-- v2.4 money first ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- When a program last became active. "New" alerts use it, not created_at, so going live is news
-- (audit C12). Set by upsertProgram; programs active before v2.4 count from created_at.
ALTER TABLE programs ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS opened_at timestamptz;
UPDATE programs SET opened_at = created_at WHERE opened_at IS NULL AND status = 'active';

-- One installation per OAuth client (spec §5): client_key = first 32 hex of sha256(client_id).
-- Installations from before v2.4 keep NULL and keep working by token.
ALTER TABLE installations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS client_key text;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS installations_client_key
  ON installations (client_key) WHERE client_key IS NOT NULL;

-- Alerts (spec §4): what this installation was last told, and the one-time daily-check offer.
ALTER TABLE installations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS alerts_checked_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE installations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS alert_state jsonb;          -- {"top": {"<project id>": true}}
ALTER TABLE installations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS fee_alerted_usd numeric;    -- fees last shown, USD
ALTER TABLE installations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS schedule_offered_at timestamptz;

-- Every wallet an installation has used (entries, creator wallets, named in list_programs):
-- its wins and fees show for them too, even from a new connection (spec §5).
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS installation_wallets (
  installation_id integer NOT NULL REFERENCES installations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  wallet          text NOT NULL,                       -- lowercase
  first_seen_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY (installation_id, wallet)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS installation_wallets_wallet ON installation_wallets (wallet);
INSERT INTO installation_wallets (installation_id, wallet)
  SELECT id, lower(payout_wallet) FROM installations WHERE payout_wallet IS NOT NULL
  ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING;

-- Token retry (spec §6, audit C14): the creator wallet given at record_submission or used by a
-- launch. launch_token reuses it; token-fee reads (FeeLocker) use it.
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS creator_wallet text;

-- Entry proof and scoring v2 (spec §2, §3).
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS deployers text[];      -- each entered contract's deployer, lowercase
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS score_error text;      -- the last scoring failure; NULL once scored
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS code_share real;       -- share of direct txs carrying EarnKit's Builder Code
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entry_wallets (
  project_id integer NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  wallet     text NOT NULL,                                      -- lowercase
  kind       text NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('eoa', 'smart')),
  PRIMARY KEY (project_id, wallet)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS entry_wallets_wallet ON entry_wallets (wallet);

-- The automatic lifecycle of EarnKit campaigns (spec §3): the final score, review vetoes, and an
-- idempotent payout (the signed transfer is stored before it is broadcast).
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS score_final boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS vetoed_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS veto_reason text;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payout_status text;
ALTER TABLE projects DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS projects_payout_status_check;
ALTER TABLE projects ADD CONSTRAINT projects_payout_status_check
  CHECK (payout_status IN ('sending', 'paid', 'refused'));
ALTER TABLE projects ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS prize_raw_tx text;   -- the signed transfer until it is mined
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS campaign_runs (
  program_id   text PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES programs(id),
  state        text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running'
               CHECK (state IN ('running', 'review', 'paying', 'paid')),
  frozen_at    timestamptz,
  review_until timestamptz,
  note         text,                                   -- shown on the campaign page (e.g. wallet short)
  updated_at   timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- Token fees waiting in Clanker's FeeLocker (WETH), per creator wallet, cached 6 h (spec §4).
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS token_fees (
  wallet   text PRIMARY KEY,                           -- lowercase
  weth     text NOT NULL,                              -- wei, decimal string
  usd      numeric NOT NULL,
  read_at  timestamptz NOT NULL
);
```

```ts
/**
 * The installation key of an OAuth client (v2.4 §5): the first 32 hex of sha256(client_id).
 * Re-authorizing the same client maps to the same installation. Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export const clientKeyOf = (clientId: string) =>
  createHash('sha256').update(clientId).digest('hex').slice(0, 32);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Installations and the token route.** In `installations.ts`, replace `installationExists` and `saveInstallation` (and drop the stray doc line above them):

```ts
/** True when this token, or (v2.4) this OAuth client, already has an installation. */
export async function installationExists(token: string, clientKey?: string) {
  const [row] = await sql()`
    SELECT 1 FROM installations
    WHERE token_hash = ${hash(token)} OR (${clientKey ?? null}::text IS NOT NULL AND client_key = ${clientKey ?? null})`;
  return Boolean(row);
}

/**
 * Records the installation for a newly issued token. With a client key (v2.4 §5) there is one
 * installation per OAuth client: a new token for a known client replaces its token (the old one
 * stops working), so entries, wins and memory carry over. Idempotent for replayed codes.
 */
export async function saveInstallation(token: string, clientKey?: string) {
  if (!clientKey) {
    await sql()`INSERT INTO installations (token_hash) VALUES (${hash(token)}) ON CONFLICT (token_hash) DO NOTHING`;
    return;
  }
  await sql()`
    INSERT INTO installations (token_hash, client_key) VALUES (${hash(token)}, ${clientKey})
    ON CONFLICT (client_key) WHERE client_key IS NOT NULL
    DO UPDATE SET token_hash = EXCLUDED.token_hash`;
}

/**
 * Remembers wallets this installation used (lowercase; anything that isn't a 0x address is
 * ignored). Its wins and token fees show for them too (v2.4 §5).
 */
export async function rememberWallets(
  installationId: number,
  wallets: (string | null | undefined)[],
) {
  const list = [
    ...new Set(
      wallets
        .filter((w): w is string => !!w && /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(w))
        .map((w) => w.toLowerCase()),
    ),
  ];
  if (list.length === 0) return;
  await sql()`
    INSERT INTO installation_wallets ${sql()(list.map((wallet) => ({ installation_id: installationId, wallet })))}
    ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`;
}

/** The wallets this installation has used (lowercase). */
export async function installationWallets(installationId: number) {
  const rows = await sql()<{ wallet: string }[]>`
    SELECT wallet FROM installation_wallets WHERE installation_id = ${installationId} ORDER BY first_seen_at`;
  return rows.map((r) => r.wallet);
}
```

```ts
    // opened_at: set when the program becomes active, kept while it stays active (v2.4 §4).

        opened_at = CASE
          WHEN EXCLUDED.status <> 'active' THEN programs.opened_at
          WHEN programs.status = 'active' THEN COALESCE(programs.opened_at, EXCLUDED.opened_at)
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The migration check.** Create `apps/frontend/scripts/db-check.mts`:

```ts
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

/**
 * v2.4: db/schema.sql runs before every deploy, so it must be idempotent and safe on the rows
 * production already has. Against a throwaway local Postgres only:
 *   DATABASE_URL=postgres://postgres@127.0.0.1:55442/postgres?sslmode=disable pnpm db:check
 * Drops the public schema, applies main's schema (d924e4a), adds rows like production's, applies
 * the current schema twice, and checks the backfills, the defaults and opened_at through sync.
 */
function fail(message: string): never {
  console.error(`FAIL: ${message}`);
  process.exit(1);
}
let host = '';
try {
  host = new URL(process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? '').hostname;
} catch {
  fail('DATABASE_URL is missing or not a URL.');
}
if (host !== '127.0.0.1' && host !== 'localhost')
  fail('DATABASE_URL must point at a local Postgres (this script drops the public schema).');

const { sql } = await import('../lib/earnkit/db');
const db = sql();
const root = join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..', '..');
const before = execFileSync('git', ['-C', root, 'show', 'd924e4a:apps/frontend/db/schema.sql'], {
  encoding: 'utf8',
});
const now = readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, '..', 'db', 'schema.sql'), 'utf8');
let checks = 0;
const ok = (cond: unknown, what: string) => {
  if (!cond) fail(what);
  checks++;
  console.log(`ok: ${what}`);
};
const W = '0x' + 'a'.repeat(40);

await db.unsafe('DROP SCHEMA public CASCADE; CREATE SCHEMA public;');
await db.unsafe(before);
await db.unsafe(`
  INSERT INTO programs (id, organizer, title, description, source_url, submission_url, reward, summary, status, existing_work, created_at)
  VALUES ('old-active', 'X', 'Old', 'd', 'https://x.example', 'https://x.example', 'r', 's', 'active', 'allowed', '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z'),
         ('old-closed', 'X', 'Closed', 'd', 'https://x.example', 'https://x.example', 'r', 's', 'closed', 'allowed', '2026-09-01T00:00:00Z');
  INSERT INTO installations (token_hash, payout_wallet) VALUES ('h1', '${W}'), ('h2', NULL);
  INSERT INTO projects (program_id, name, installation_id, payout_wallet, contracts, score)
  VALUES ('old-active', 'P', 1, '${W}', '{}', 12);`);
await db.unsafe(now);
await db.unsafe(now); // every deploy runs it again

const [active] = await db<{ opened_at: Date | null }[]>`SELECT opened_at FROM programs WHERE id = 'old-active'`;
ok(active.opened_at?.toISOString() === '2026-09-01T00:00:00.000Z', 'an active program gets opened_at = created_at');
const [closed] = await db<{ opened_at: Date | null }[]>`SELECT opened_at FROM programs WHERE id = 'old-closed'`;
ok(closed.opened_at === null, 'a closed program keeps opened_at NULL');
const [w] = await db<{ n: number }[]>`SELECT count(*)::int AS n FROM installation_wallets WHERE wallet = ${W}`;
ok(w.n === 1, "an installation's old payout wallet is one of its wallets");
const [p] = await db<{ score: number; score_final: boolean; payout_status: string | null; creator_wallet: string | null }[]>`
  SELECT score, score_final, payout_status, creator_wallet FROM projects`;
ok(p.score === 12 && p.score_final === false && p.payout_status === null && p.creator_wallet === null, 'existing entries keep their score and get safe defaults');
const [i] = await db<{ client_key: string | null }[]>`SELECT client_key FROM installations WHERE token_hash = 'h1'`;
ok(i.client_key === null, 'installations from before v2.4 keep working by token');
await db`UPDATE installations SET client_key = 'k1' WHERE token_hash = 'h1'`;
let duplicate = false;
try {
  await db`UPDATE installations SET client_key = 'k1' WHERE token_hash = 'h2'`;
} catch {
  duplicate = true;
}
ok(duplicate, 'two installations can never share an OAuth client');

// opened_at follows status through programs:sync.
const { upsertProgram } = await import('../lib/earnkit/programs');
const { ProgramFile } = await import('../lib/earnkit/program-file');
const file = ProgramFile.parse({
  ...JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'programs', 'arc-microgrants.json'), 'utf8')),
  id: 'sync-check',
  status: 'closed',
});
const openedAt = async () =>
  (await db<{ opened_at: Date | null }[]>`SELECT opened_at FROM programs WHERE id = 'sync-check'`)[0].opened_at;
await upsertProgram(file);
ok((await openedAt()) === null, 'a closed program has no opened_at');
await upsertProgram({ ...file, status: 'active' });
const first = await openedAt();
ok(first !== null, 'going active sets opened_at');
await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 20));
await upsertProgram({ ...file, status: 'active' });
ok((await openedAt())?.getTime() === first!.getTime(), 'a later sync of an active program keeps opened_at');
await upsertProgram({ ...file, status: 'closed' });
await upsertProgram({ ...file, status: 'active' });
ok((await openedAt())!.getTime() > first!.getTime(), 'reopening a program is news again');

await db.end();
console.log(`db check: ${checks} checks passed`);
```

In the root `package.json` scripts, add `"db:check": "tsx apps/frontend/scripts/db-check.mts",` after `db:migrate`.

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { ceilSig, earnkitTexts, goLive } from './campaign-text';
import { ProgramFile } from './program-file';
import type { EarnKitCampaign } from './campaigns';

const campaign: EarnKitCampaign = {
  kind: 'earnkit',
  prize: { token: '0x534b7aAD1Cdb6F02eC48CAbe428f0D9131E40B07', symbol: 'MINI', decimals: 18, chain: 'base' },
  winners: 5,
  amount_each: '84800000',
  prize_usd_total: 500,
  priced_at: '2026-10-04',
  metric: 'unique_wallets',
  min_score: 10,
};
const window = { starts_at: '2026-10-04T12:00:00Z', ends_at: '2026-10-18T12:00:00Z' };
const FILE = join(import.meta.dirname, '../../../../programs/earnkit-campaign-1-base.json');

test('earnkitTexts: the reward, summary and rules come from the fields, money first', () => {
  const t = earnkitTexts({ ...window, campaign });
  assert.equal(t.reward, '$500 in MINI: $100 each to the top 5 (84,800,000 MINI each)');
  assert.equal(t.summary, '$100 each to top 5 (in MINI) · closes Oct 18');
  assert.deepEqual(t.rules, [
    'Agents only: the project is built and entered by an AI agent through EarnKit.',
    'Build on Base mainnet. Every contract you enter must be deployed on or after Oct 4, 2026, 12:00 UTC, from your payout wallet or from a wallet that signs your entry.',
    "Score: unique users of your contracts from Oct 4, 2026, 12:00 UTC to Oct 18, 2026, 12:00 UTC: wallets that send them successful transactions, plus smart accounts and contracts that call them. A wallet counts only if it had history before the start (3 or more transactions, or a smart account that already existed). Your payout and deployer wallets don't count.",
    'At least 10 users to be eligible. The top 5 eligible entries win; ties go to the earlier entry. One prize per payout wallet and per agent: the next entry takes the slot.',
    'After the end, the top entries and their evidence are public for 48 hours. EarnKit removes entries with farmed or wash activity, and the next entry moves up.',
    'Prize: 84,800,000 MINI per winner ($100 at the MINI price on 2026-10-04), paid automatically on Base to the payout wallet you enter, by Oct 20.',
    'One entry per repo and per contract. Up to 5 contracts per entry.',
  ]);
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(t).match(/token/i), 'no token text in a campaign\'s rules');
});

test('ceilSig rounds up to 3 significant digits', () => {
  assert.equal(ceilSig(84_745_762.7), 84_800_000);
  assert.equal(ceilSig(85_000_000), 85_000_000);
  assert.equal(ceilSig(0.123456), 0.124);
  assert.throws(() => ceilSig(0));
});

test('goLive: 14 days from now, MINI priced now, active, texts regenerated, no token_policy added', () => {
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(FILE, 'utf8'));
  const next = goLive(raw, { now: new Date('2026-10-05T09:30:41Z'), price: 0.00000118 });
  assert.equal(next.status, 'active');
  assert.equal(next.starts_at, '2026-10-05T09:30:00Z');
  assert.equal(next.ends_at, '2026-10-19T09:30:00Z');
  assert.equal(next.verified_at, '2026-10-05');
  const c = next.campaign as EarnKitCampaign;
  assert.equal(c.amount_each, '84800000');
  assert.equal(c.priced_at, '2026-10-05');
  assert.equal(next.summary, '$100 each to top 5 (in MINI) · closes Oct 19');
  assert.ok(!('token_policy' in next));
  assert.equal(ProgramFile.safeParse(next).success, true);
});

test('Campaign #1 file: valid, and its texts are the generated ones (pnpm programs:check enforces it)', () => {
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(FILE, 'utf8'));
  assert.equal(ProgramFile.safeParse(raw).success, true);
  assert.equal(raw.status, 'closed', 'stays closed until the owner runs golive');
  assert.equal(raw.max_award_usd, 100);
  assert.equal(raw.campaign.prize_usd_total, 500);
  assert.ok(!('token_policy' in raw));
  // A hand edit of the dates without regenerating the rules is refused.
  const edited = ProgramFile.safeParse({ ...raw, ends_at: '2026-11-30T00:00:00Z' });
  assert.equal(edited.success, false);
  assert.match(JSON.stringify(edited.error?.issues), /generated/);
});
```

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { llamaPrice, type PriceFetch } from './prices';

const MINI = 'base:0x534b7aAD1Cdb6F02eC48CAbe428f0D9131E40B07';
const answer = (status: number, body: unknown): PriceFetch => async () => ({
  ok: status >= 200 && status < 300,
  status,
  json: async () => body,
});

test('llamaPrice reads the coin (case-insensitive key) and its time', async () => {
  const urls: string[] = [];
  const r = await llamaPrice(MINI, async (url) => {
    urls.push(url);
    return answer(200, { coins: { [MINI.toLowerCase()]: { price: 0.00000118, timestamp: 1_791_158_400 } } })(url);
  });
  assert.equal(urls[0], `https://coins.llama.fi/prices/current/${MINI}`);
  assert.equal(r.price, 0.00000118);
  assert.equal(r.at.toISOString(), new Date(1_791_158_400_000).toISOString());
});

test('llamaPrice refuses a missing coin, a zero price and a failed request', async () => {
  await assert.rejects(llamaPrice(MINI, answer(200, { coins: {} })), /no price/);
  await assert.rejects(llamaPrice(MINI, answer(200, { coins: { [MINI]: { price: 0 } } })), /no price/);
  await assert.rejects(llamaPrice(MINI, answer(502, {})), /502/);
});
```

Update `amount_each`'s doc to `/** Whole tokens per winner ("84800000"): the USD prize at the go-live price (pnpm campaign golive). */`. After the `CampaignConfig` type, add `export type EarnKitCampaign = z.infer<typeof EarnKitCampaign>;`. Replace `prizeText` and add the helpers:

```ts
/** "$5,000": whole dollars (cents only under $10). Pure; exported for tests. */
export const usd = (n: number) =>
  `$${n.toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: n < 10 ? 2 : 0 })}`;

/** USD per winner of an EarnKit campaign. Pure. */
export const prizeUsdEach = (c: EarnKitCampaign) => c.prize_usd_total / c.winners;

export const prizeText = (c: CampaignConfig) =>
  c.kind === 'earnkit'
    ? `${usd(prizeUsdEach(c))} each to the top ${c.winners} (${fmt(c.amount_each)} ${c.prize.symbol} each)`
    : `${fmt(c.pool)} ${c.prize.symbol} pool (${fmt(c.prize_total)} to winners)`;

/** list_programs' prize column (v2.4 §1): "$100 each to top 5 (in MINI)". Pure. */
export const prizeColumn = (c: CampaignConfig) =>
  c.kind === 'earnkit'
    ? `${usd(prizeUsdEach(c))} each to top ${c.winners} (in ${c.prize.symbol})`
    : prizeText(c);

/** What one winner got, money first: "$100 in MINI" for EarnKit campaigns, else wonText. Pure. */
export const wonUsdText = (c: CampaignConfig, prizeAmount: string | null) =>
  c.kind === 'earnkit'
    ? `${usd(prizeUsdEach(c))} in ${c.prize.symbol}`
    : wonText(c, prizeAmount);
```

```ts
/** The public review after an EarnKit campaign ends, before its automatic payout (v2.4 §3). */
export const REVIEW_MS = 48 * 3_600_000;

/**

 */
```

```ts
/** "Oct 18": a short UTC day for tables and builder text. */
export function shortDate(d: Date | string) {
  const t = typeof d === 'string' ? new Date(d) : d;
  return `${MONTHS[t.getUTCMonth()]} ${t.getUTCDate()}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `prices.ts`.**

```ts
/** A fetch as llamaPrice uses it; injectable for tests. */
export type PriceFetch = (
  url: string,
) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; status: number; json: () => Promise<unknown> }>;

const defaultFetch: PriceFetch = (url) =>
  fetch(url, {
    headers: { accept: 'application/json', 'user-agent': 'earnkit' },
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),
  });

/**
 * The current USD price of `coin` ("<chain>:<address>", e.g. "base:0x534b…") from DefiLlama's free
 * price API (no key; v2.4 cost rule). Throws when DefiLlama has no positive price for it.
 */
export async function llamaPrice(coin: string, fetchImpl: PriceFetch = defaultFetch) {
  const res = await fetchImpl(`https://coins.llama.fi/prices/current/${coin}`);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`DefiLlama answered ${res.status} for ${coin}`);
  const body = (await res.json()) as {
    coins?: Record<string, { price?: number; timestamp?: number }>;
  };
  const hit = Object.entries(body.coins ?? {}).find(
    ([k]) => k.toLowerCase() === coin.toLowerCase(),
  )?.[1];
  if (!hit || typeof hit.price !== 'number' || !(hit.price > 0))
    throw new Error(`DefiLlama has no price for ${coin}`);
  return { price: hit.price, at: new Date((hit.timestamp ?? Date.now() / 1000) * 1000) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `campaign-text.ts`.**

```ts
import { formatDeadline, shortDate } from './dates';
import { payoutBy, prizeUsdEach, usd, type EarnKitCampaign } from './campaigns';
import type { ProgramFile } from './program-file';

const tokens = (n: string) => Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US');

/**
 * An EarnKit campaign's reward, summary and rules, generated from its fields (v2.4 §3) so dates and
 * amounts are never hand-written. programs:check refuses a file whose texts differ; pnpm campaign
 * golive writes them. No token text: tokens are never advertised. Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export function earnkitTexts(p: {
  starts_at: string | null;
  ends_at: string | null;
  campaign: EarnKitCampaign;
}) {
  const c = p.campaign;
  const starts = new Date(p.starts_at!);
  const ends = new Date(p.ends_at!);
  const each = usd(prizeUsdEach(c));
  const amount = `${tokens(c.amount_each)} ${c.prize.symbol}`;
  return {
    reward: `${usd(c.prize_usd_total)} in ${c.prize.symbol}: ${each} each to the top ${c.winners} (${amount} each)`,
    summary: `${each} each to top ${c.winners} (in ${c.prize.symbol}) · closes ${shortDate(ends)}`,
    rules: [
      'Agents only: the project is built and entered by an AI agent through EarnKit.',
      `Build on Base mainnet. Every contract you enter must be deployed on or after ${formatDeadline(starts)}, from your payout wallet or from a wallet that signs your entry.`,
      `Score: unique users of your contracts from ${formatDeadline(starts)} to ${formatDeadline(ends)}: wallets that send them successful transactions, plus smart accounts and contracts that call them. A wallet counts only if it had history before the start (3 or more transactions, or a smart account that already existed). Your payout and deployer wallets don't count.`,
      `At least ${c.min_score} users to be eligible. The top ${c.winners} eligible entries win; ties go to the earlier entry. One prize per payout wallet and per agent: the next entry takes the slot.`,
      'After the end, the top entries and their evidence are public for 48 hours. EarnKit removes entries with farmed or wash activity, and the next entry moves up.',
      `Prize: ${amount} per winner (${each} at the ${c.prize.symbol} price${c.priced_at ? ` on ${c.priced_at}` : ''}), paid automatically on Base to the payout wallet you enter, by ${shortDate(payoutBy(ends))}.`,
      'One entry per repo and per contract. Up to 5 contracts per entry.',
    ],
  };
}

/** Rounds up to `digits` significant digits: 84,745,763 -> 84,800,000. Pure; exported for tests. */
export function ceilSig(x: number, digits = 3) {
  if (!(x > 0)) throw new Error('ceilSig needs a positive number');
  const step = 10 ** (Math.floor(Math.log10(x)) - digits + 1);
  return Number((Math.ceil(x / step - 1e-9) * step).toPrecision(digits + 1));
}

/** ISO without milliseconds ("2026-10-05T09:30:00Z"), the way program files write times. */
const iso = (d: Date) => d.toISOString().replace('.000Z', 'Z');

/**
 * The file as it goes live (v2.4 §3): starts now (to the minute), ends `days` later (default 14),
 * amount_each = the USD prize per winner at `price`, rounded up to 3 significant digits, priced
 * today, status active, texts regenerated. Keys the file doesn't have are not added. Pure.
 */
export function goLive(file: ProgramFile, a: { now: Date; price: number; days?: number }): ProgramFile {
  if (file.campaign?.kind !== 'earnkit') throw new Error(`${file.id} isn't an EarnKit campaign.`);
  const start = new Date(Math.floor(a.now.getTime() / 60_000) * 60_000);
  const end = new Date(start.getTime() + (a.days ?? 14) * 86_400_000);
  const today = a.now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const campaign: EarnKitCampaign = {
    ...file.campaign,
    amount_each: String(ceilSig(prizeUsdEach(file.campaign) / a.price)),
    priced_at: today,
  };
  const starts_at = iso(start);
  const ends_at = iso(end);
  return {
    ...file,
    ...earnkitTexts({ starts_at, ends_at, campaign }),
    status: 'active',
    starts_at,
    ends_at,
    verified_at: today,
    campaign,
  };
}
```

Check `ceilSig(0.123456)`: step = 10^(−1−3+1) = 0.001; 123.456 → ceil 124 → 0.124. `toPrecision` removes float noise.

In `scripts/campaign.mts`:

- at the top of `main()`, add `if (command === 'golive') return goLiveCommand();`;
- append:

```ts
/** golive <slug>: edits programs/<slug>.json only (no database); the owner commits and pushes it. */
async function goLiveCommand() {
  const { readFileSync, writeFileSync } = await import('node:fs');
  const { join } = await import('node:path');
  const { ProgramFile } = await import('../lib/earnkit/program-file');
  const { earnkitTexts, goLive } = await import('../lib/earnkit/campaign-text');
  const path = join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..', '..', 'programs', `${slug}.json`);
  const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, 'utf8'));
  if (raw.campaign?.kind !== 'earnkit') fail(`${slug} isn't an EarnKit campaign.`);
  let next: typeof raw;
  if (process.argv.includes('--texts-only')) {
    next = { ...raw, ...earnkitTexts(raw) };
  } else {
    const days = Number(flag('days') ?? 14);
    if (!Number.isInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 60)
      fail('--days must be a whole number from 1 to 60.');
    const { llamaPrice } = await import('../lib/earnkit/prices');
    const { price } = await llamaPrice(`base:${raw.campaign.prize.token}`);
    next = goLive(raw, { now: new Date(), price, days });
    const c = next.campaign;
    const total = BigInt(c.amount_each) * BigInt(c.winners);
    console.log(
      `${next.title}\n  ${next.starts_at} -> ${next.ends_at}\n` +
        `  ${c.prize.symbol} at $${price}: ${Number(c.amount_each).toLocaleString('en-US')} each\n` +
        `  Fund the payout wallet with ${total.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${c.prize.symbol} plus a little ETH for gas.`,
    );
  }
  const parsed = ProgramFile.safeParse(next);
  if (!parsed.success)
    fail(`The new file isn't valid: ${parsed.error.issues.map((i) => i.message).join('; ')}`);
  if (!process.argv.includes('--write')) {
    console.log('\nDry run. Add --write to update the file, then commit and push it.');
    return;
  }
  // The raw object, not the parsed one: parsing adds defaults (token_policy) the file leaves out.
  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(next, null, 2) + '\n');
  console.log(`\nWrote programs/${slug}.json. Commit and push it to go live.`);
}
```

`main()` returns before `db.end()` for golive; the outer code then calls `process.exit`. Check that the top-level `try { db = sql(); await main(); }` is followed by an exit. If it isn't, add `process.exit(0)` after `await main()` for golive only: `if (command === 'golive') process.exit(0);`.

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { Attribution } from 'ox/erc8021';
import { EARNKIT_BUILDER_CODE, codesIn } from './builder-code';

test("codesIn reads ERC-8021 codes off a transaction's input; anything else is none", () => {
  const suffix = Attribution.toDataSuffix({ codes: ['earnkit', 'bc_ab12cd34'] });
  const transfer = '0xa9059cbb' + '00'.repeat(64);
  assert.deepEqual(codesIn(transfer + suffix.slice(2)), ['earnkit', 'bc_ab12cd34']);
  assert.deepEqual(codesIn(transfer), []);
  assert.deepEqual(codesIn('0x'), []);
  assert.deepEqual(codesIn(undefined), []);
  assert.deepEqual(codesIn('0xzz'), []);
});

test("EarnKit's code defaults to earnkit", () => {

});
```

```ts
const at = (h: number) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 5, h)).toISOString();
const page = <T,>(items: T[]) => ({ items, next_page_params: null });

test('internalCallers: successful calls inside the window only', async () => {
  const r = await internalCallers('0xc', new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 5, 1)), new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 5, 20)), {
    fetchPage: async (path) => {
      assert.match(path, /^\/api\/v2\/addresses\/0xc\/internal-transactions\?filter=to$/);
      return page([
        { timestamp: at(10), success: true, type: 'call', from: { hash: '0xAA' } },
        { timestamp: at(9), success: false, type: 'call', from: { hash: '0xbb' } },
        { timestamp: at(8), success: true, type: 'delegatecall', from: { hash: '0xcc' } },
        { timestamp: at(21), success: true, type: 'call', from: { hash: '0xdd' } },
        { timestamp: at(0), success: true, type: 'call', from: { hash: '0xee' } },
      ]) as InternalPage;
    },
  });
  assert.deepEqual([...r.wallets], ['0xaa']);
  assert.equal(r.complete, true);
});

test('walletHistory: EOAs need 3+ transactions before the start; smart accounts must have existed', async () => {
  const rpc = (code: { now: string; then: string }, nonce: number) =>
    ({
      getCode: async ({ blockNumber }: { blockNumber?: bigint }) => (blockNumber === undefined ? code.now : code.then),
      getTransactionCount: async () => nonce,
    }) as never;
  assert.equal(await walletHistory('0x1', 5n, rpc({ now: '0x', then: '0x' }, 2)), null);
  assert.equal(await walletHistory('0x1', 5n, rpc({ now: '0x', then: '0x' }, 3)), 'eoa');
  assert.equal(await walletHistory('0x1', 5n, rpc({ now: '0x6080', then: '0x6080' }, 0)), 'smart');
  assert.equal(await walletHistory('0x1', 5n, rpc({ now: '0x6080', then: '0x' }, 0)), null);
  // An EIP-7702 delegated EOA has code "0xef0100<address>": still an EOA.
  assert.equal(await walletHistory('0x1', 5n, rpc({ now: '0xef0100' + 'ab'.repeat(20), then: '0x' }, 4)), 'eoa');
});

test('scoreEntry v2: internal callers count; the payout wallet, deployers, contracts and EntryPoints never do', async () => {
  const C = '0x' + 'c'.repeat(40);
  const USER = '0x' + '1'.repeat(40);
  const SMART = '0x' + '2'.repeat(40);
  const PAYOUT = '0x' + '3'.repeat(40);
  const DEPLOYER = '0x' + '4'.repeat(40);
  const NEW = '0x' + '5'.repeat(40);
  const from = new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 5, 0));
  const to = new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 6, 0));
  const r = await scoreEntry([C], from, to, {
    blockAt: async () => 1n,
    fetchPage: async () =>
      page([USER, PAYOUT, DEPLOYER, NEW, C].map((w, i) => ({ timestamp: at(10 + i), status: 'ok', from: { hash: w } }))) as TxPage,
    fetchInternal: async () =>
      page([SMART, ENTRY_POINTS[1]].map((w) => ({ timestamp: at(12), success: true, type: 'call', from: { hash: w } }))) as InternalPage,
    history: async (w) => (w === SMART ? 'smart' : w === NEW ? null : 'eoa'),
    exclude: [PAYOUT, DEPLOYER.toUpperCase().replace('0X', '0x')],
  });
  assert.deepEqual(r.counted.sort((a, b) => a.wallet.localeCompare(b.wallet)), [
    { wallet: USER, kind: 'eoa' },
    { wallet: SMART, kind: 'smart' },
  ]);
  assert.equal(r.score, 2);
  assert.equal(r.seen, 3); // USER, NEW, SMART: the excluded ones are never looked at
});
```

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { scoreCampaignEntries, type ScoreRow } from './campaign-score';

const program = { starts_at: new Date('2026-10-05T00:00:00Z'), ends_at: new Date('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z') };
const row = (id: number, extra: Partial<ScoreRow> = {}): ScoreRow => ({
  id,
  contracts: [`0x${String(id).repeat(40)}`],
  payout_wallet: `0x${'a'.repeat(39)}${id}`,
  deployers: [`0x${'d'.repeat(39)}${id}`],
  score: null,
  scored_at: null,
  scored_through: null,
  score_final: false,
  ...extra,
});
const result = (score: number) => ({
  score, wallets: [], counted: [], seen: score, complete: true, timedOut: false, codeShare: null,
});

test('v2.4 §6: one failing entry never stops the others; its error is stored on it', async () => {
  const stored: number[] = [];
  const failed: [number, string][] = [];
  const out = await scoreCampaignEntries(
    program,
    [row(1), row(2), row(3)],
    { now: new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z'), deadline: Date.now() + 5_000, final: false, maxAgeMs: 0 },
    {
      score: async (contracts) => {
        if (contracts[0] === row(2).contracts[0]) throw new Error('Base explorer /api/v2/addresses returned 502');
        return result(4);
      },
      store: async (id) => { stored.push(id); },
      fail: async (id, m) => { failed.push([id, m]); },
    },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(stored, [1, 3]);
  assert.deepEqual(failed, [[2, 'Base explorer /api/v2/addresses returned 502']]);
  assert.deepEqual(out.map((o) => o.result), ['scored', 'failed', 'scored']);
});

test("the entry's payout wallet and deployers are excluded; the window ends at now while running", async () => {
  const calls: { to: Date; exclude?: string[] }[] = [];
  await scoreCampaignEntries(
    program,
    [row(1)],
    { now: new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z'), deadline: Date.now() + 5_000, final: false, maxAgeMs: 0 },
    {
      score: async (_c, _f, to, opts) => { calls.push({ to, exclude: opts?.exclude }); return result(1); },
      store: async () => {},
      fail: async () => {},
    },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ to: new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z'), exclude: [row(1).payout_wallet, ...row(1).deployers!] }]);
});

test('final: the whole window once, marked final; entries already final are skipped', async () => {
  const stored: { id: number; through: Date | null; final: boolean }[] = [];
  const out = await scoreCampaignEntries(
    program,
    [row(1), row(2, { score: 5, score_final: true })],
    { now: new Date('2026-10-19T00:20:00Z'), deadline: Date.now() + 5_000, final: true, maxAgeMs: 0 },
    {
      score: async () => result(7),
      store: async (id, _r, _at, through, final) => { stored.push({ id, through, final }); },
      fail: async () => {},
    },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(stored, [{ id: 1, through: program.ends_at, final: true }]);
  assert.deepEqual(out.map((o) => o.result), ['final', 'skipped']);
});

test('a fresh running score is skipped; a read cut short saves a provisional score only where there is none', async () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-10-06T00:00:00Z');
  const fresh = row(1, { score: 3, scored_at: new Date('2026-10-05T23:00:00Z'), scored_through: new Date('2026-10-05T23:00:00Z') });
  const stored: { id: number; through: Date | null }[] = [];
  const out = await scoreCampaignEntries(
    program,
    [fresh, row(2)],
    { now, deadline: Date.now() + 5_000, final: false, maxAgeMs: 20 * 3_600_000 },
    {
      score: async () => ({ ...result(1), timedOut: true, complete: false }),
      store: async (id, _r, _at, through) => { stored.push({ id, through }); },
      fail: async () => {},
    },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(out.map((o) => o.result), ['skipped', 'timed_out']);
  assert.deepEqual(stored, [{ id: 2, through: null }]);
});
```

```ts
import { Attribution } from 'ox/erc8021';

/**
 * EarnKit's Base Builder Code (ERC-8021), registered once by the owner at base.dev (v2.4 §7). The
 * campaign starter appends it to every transaction; the review shows the share of a top entry's
 * transactions carrying it (supporting evidence only: scoring never depends on it). Confirm the
 * exact string with the owner before launch; EARNKIT_BUILDER_CODE overrides it.
 */
export const EARNKIT_BUILDER_CODE = process.env.EARNKIT_BUILDER_CODE?.trim() || 'earnkit';

/** The ERC-8021 codes at the end of a transaction's input, or [] (none, or unreadable). Pure; exported for tests. */
export function codesIn(input: string | null | undefined): string[] {
  if (!input || !/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]*$/.test(input) || input.length < 2 + 2 * 18) return [];
  try {
    const a = Attribution.fromData(input as `0x${string}`) as
      | { codes?: readonly string[]; appCode?: string }
      | undefined;
    return a?.codes ? [...a.codes] : a?.appCode ? [a.appCode] : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}
```

```ts
const BLOCKSCOUT = 'https://base.blockscout.com';

/**
 * Base Blockscout, or EARNKIT_BLOCKSCOUT_URL when NODE_ENV is exactly development or test (the
 * campaign e2e serves a mock explorer); refused otherwise, like EARNKIT_OWNERSHIP_BASE_URL.
 */
export function blockscoutBase(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env) {
  const override = env.EARNKIT_BLOCKSCOUT_URL;
  if (!override) return BLOCKSCOUT;
  if (env.NODE_ENV !== 'development' && env.NODE_ENV !== 'test')
    throw new Error('EARNKIT_BLOCKSCOUT_URL is for development and tests only; unset it.');
  return override.replace(/\/+$/, '');
}
```

After `walletsUsing`, add:

```ts
/** ERC-4337 EntryPoints (v0.6, v0.7, v0.8), lowercase: never users (v2.4 §3). */
export const ENTRY_POINTS = [
  '0x5ff137d4b0fdcd49dca30c7cf57e578a026d2789',
  '0x0000000071727de22e5e9d8baf0edac6f37da032',
  '0x4337084d9e255ff0702461cf8895ce9e3b5ff108',
];

export type InternalPage = {
  items: { timestamp: string; success?: boolean; type?: string; from: { hash: string } }[];
  next_page_params: Record<string, unknown> | null;
};

/**
 * Smart accounts and contracts that called `contract` (successful internal calls of type "call")
 * within the window, newest first: Base App and relayed users arrive this way (v2.4 §3). Always
 * read from now back (Blockscout's internal list has its own cursor); `complete` is false when
 * `maxPages` ran out first.
 */
export async function internalCallers(
  contract: string,
  from: Date,
  to: Date,
  opts: {
    maxPages?: number;
    deadline?: number;
    fetchPage?: (path: string) => Promise<InternalPage>;
  } = {},
) {
  const fetchPage =
    opts.fetchPage ?? ((path: string) => blockscout<InternalPage>(path, opts.deadline));
  const wallets = new Set<string>();
  let params = firstPageParams();
  for (let n = 0; n < (opts.maxPages ?? SCORE_MAX_PAGES); n++) {
    if (opts.deadline !== undefined && Date.now() >= opts.deadline) throw new ScoreDeadline();
    const data = await fetchPage(`/api/v2/addresses/${contract}/internal-transactions?${params}`);
    for (const it of data.items) {
      const at = new Date(it.timestamp);
      if (at < from) return { wallets, complete: true };
      if (at <= to && it.success === true && (it.type ?? 'call') === 'call')
        wallets.add(it.from.hash.toLowerCase());
    }
    if (!data.next_page_params) return { wallets, complete: true };
    params = new URLSearchParams(
      Object.entries(data.next_page_params).map(([k, v]) => [k, String(v)]),
    ).toString();
  }
  return { wallets, complete: false };
}
```

Replace `hadHistoryBefore` with:

```ts
/** Transactions an EOA must have sent before the start to count (v2.4 §3). */
export const MIN_EOA_NONCE = 3;
export type WalletKind = 'eoa' | 'smart';

/**
 * How a wallet counts (v2.4 §3): an EOA whose nonce at `blockBefore` was at least MIN_EOA_NONCE
 * ('eoa'); a smart account (code now) that already had code at `blockBefore` ('smart'); else null.
 * An EIP-7702 delegated EOA (code "0xef0100…") is an EOA. Errors propagate.
 */
export async function walletHistory(
  wallet: string,
  blockBefore: bigint,
  rpc: BaseRpc = baseRpc,
): Promise<WalletKind | null> {
  const address = wallet as `0x${string}`;
  const code = await rpc.getCode({ address });
  if (code && code !== '0x' && !/^0xef0100/i.test(code)) {
    const then = await rpc.getCode({ address, blockNumber: blockBefore });
    return then && then !== '0x' ? 'smart' : null;
  }
  const nonce = await rpc.getTransactionCount({ address, blockNumber: blockBefore });
  return nonce >= MIN_EOA_NONCE ? 'eoa' : null;
}
```

Rewrite `scoreEntry`, keeping its doc comment but describing v2.4:

```ts
/**
 * Metric `unique_wallets` (v2.4 §3): distinct users of any of the entry's contracts in the window —
 * senders of successful transactions to them, plus smart accounts and contracts calling them
 * (internal calls) — that had history before the start (walletHistory). Never counted: `exclude`
 * (the payout wallet and deployers), the entry's own contracts and the EntryPoints. Returns the
 * score, the counted wallets with their kind, `codeShare` (the share of direct transactions
 * carrying EarnKit's Builder Code; null without any), and `complete`/`timedOut` as before. With
 * an injected `fetchPage` and no `fetchInternal`, internal calls aren't read (tests).
 */
export async function scoreEntry(
  contracts: string[],
  from: Date,
  to: Date,
  opts: {
    deadline?: number;
    fetchPage?: (path: string) => Promise<TxPage>;
    fetchInternal?: (path: string) => Promise<InternalPage>;
    blockAt?: (at: Date) => Promise<bigint>;
    history?: (wallet: string, blockBefore: bigint) => Promise<WalletKind | null>;
    exclude?: string[];
  } = {},
) {
  const { deadline } = opts;
  const rpc = baseRpcUntil(deadline);
  const blockAt = (at: Date) =>
    beforeDeadline(opts.blockAt ? opts.blockAt(at) : baseBlockAt(at, rpc), deadline);
  const history =
    opts.history ?? ((w: string, b: bigint) => walletHistory(w, b, rpc));
  const fetchInternal =
    opts.fetchInternal ??
    (opts.fetchPage
      ? async (): Promise<InternalPage> => ({ items: [], next_page_params: null })
      : undefined);
  const never = new Set(
    [...ENTRY_POINTS, ...contracts, ...(opts.exclude ?? [])].map((w) => w.toLowerCase()),
  );
  const all = new Set<string>();
  const counted: { wallet: string; kind: WalletKind }[] = [];
  let complete = true;
  let txs = 0;
  let coded = 0;
  const out = (timedOut: boolean) => ({
    score: counted.length,
    wallets: counted.map((c) => c.wallet),
    counted,
    seen: all.size,
    complete: timedOut ? false : complete,
    timedOut,
    codeShare: txs ? coded / txs : null,
  });
  try {
    const startBlock = to.getTime() < Date.now() ? (await blockAt(to)) + 1n : undefined;
    for (const c of contracts) {
      const r = await walletsUsing(c, from, to, { startBlock, deadline, fetchPage: opts.fetchPage });
      const i = await internalCallers(c, from, to, { deadline, fetchPage: fetchInternal });
      for (const w of [...r.wallets, ...i.wallets]) if (!never.has(w)) all.add(w);
      complete &&= r.complete && i.complete;
      txs += r.txs;
      coded += r.coded;
    }
    const blockBefore = await blockAt(from);
    for (const w of all) {
      if (deadline !== undefined && Date.now() >= deadline) throw new ScoreDeadline();
      const kind = await beforeDeadline(history(w, blockBefore), deadline);
      if (kind) counted.push({ wallet: w, kind });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    const timedOut =
      err instanceof ScoreDeadline || (deadline !== undefined && Date.now() >= deadline);
    if (!timedOut) throw err;
    return out(true);
  }
  return out(false);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `campaign-score.ts`.**

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import {
  needsScore,
  scoreEntry,
  type InternalPage,
  type TxPage,
  type WalletKind,
} from './campaigns';

/** What scoring reads about an entry. */
export type ScoreRow = {
  id: number;
  contracts: string[];
  payout_wallet: string;
  deployers: string[] | null;
  score: number | null;
  scored_at: Date | null;
  scored_through: Date | null;
  score_final: boolean;
};

type Scored = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof scoreEntry>>;
type StoreScore = (
  id: number,
  r: Scored,
  at: Date,
  through: Date | null,
  final: boolean,
) => Promise<void>;

/** Injection for tests and the e2e (the cron passes scoreOpts through). */
export type ScoreDeps = {
  score?: typeof scoreEntry;
  store?: StoreScore;
  fail?: (id: number, message: string) => Promise<void>;
  scoreOpts?: {
    blockAt?: (at: Date) => Promise<bigint>;
    history?: (wallet: string, blockBefore: bigint) => Promise<WalletKind | null>;
    fetchPage?: (path: string) => Promise<TxPage>;
    fetchInternal?: (path: string) => Promise<InternalPage>;
  };
};

/**
 * Stores a score with its counted wallets (entry_wallets, replaced) in one transaction (v2.4 §3);
 * clears score_error. `through` null is a provisional score (a read cut short).
 */
export const storeScore: StoreScore = async (id, r, at, through, final) => {
  await sql().begin(async (tx) => {
    await tx`
      UPDATE projects SET score = ${r.score}, scored_at = ${at}, scored_through = ${through},
        score_partial = ${!r.complete}, score_error = NULL, code_share = ${r.codeShare},
        score_final = ${final}
      WHERE id = ${id}`;
    await tx`DELETE FROM entry_wallets WHERE project_id = ${id}`;
    if (r.counted.length > 0)
      await tx`INSERT INTO entry_wallets ${tx(
        r.counted.map((c) => ({ project_id: id, wallet: c.wallet, kind: c.kind })),
      )}`;
  });
};

const storeFailure = async (id: number, message: string) => {
  await sql()`UPDATE projects SET score_error = ${message.slice(0, 300)} WHERE id = ${id}`;
};

/**
 * Scores each entry that needs it, one at a time, until `deadline` (epoch ms). One entry's failure
 * is stored on it (score_error) and never stops the others (v2.4 §6). Running: the window ends at
 * min(now, ends_at) and a score is refreshed once older than maxAgeMs. `final`: after the end, the
 * whole window once per entry, marked score_final. A read cut short by the deadline is saved only
 * where there is no score yet (provisional), and the pass stops.
 */
export async function scoreCampaignEntries(
  program: { starts_at: Date; ends_at: Date },
  entries: ScoreRow[],
  a: { now: Date; deadline: number; final: boolean; maxAgeMs: number },
  deps: ScoreDeps = {},
) {
  const score = deps.score ?? scoreEntry;
  const store = deps.store ?? storeScore;
  const fail = deps.fail ?? storeFailure;
  const out: { id: number; result: 'scored' | 'final' | 'skipped' | 'failed' | 'timed_out'; error?: string }[] = [];
  for (const e of entries) {
    const due = a.final
      ? !e.score_final
      : needsScore(e.scored_at, e.scored_through, program.ends_at, a.now, a.maxAgeMs);
    if (!due) {
      out.push({ id: e.id, result: 'skipped' });
      continue;
    }
    if (Date.now() >= a.deadline) {
      out.push({ id: e.id, result: 'timed_out' });
      continue;
    }
    const until = a.final
      ? program.ends_at
      : new Date(Math.min(a.now.getTime(), program.ends_at.getTime()));
    try {
      const r = await score(e.contracts, program.starts_at, until, {
        ...deps.scoreOpts,
        deadline: a.deadline,
        exclude: [e.payout_wallet, ...(e.deployers ?? [])],
      });
      if (r.timedOut) {
        if (e.score === null) await store(e.id, r, a.now, null, false);
        out.push({ id: e.id, result: 'timed_out' });
        continue;
      }
      await store(e.id, r, a.now, until, a.final);
      out.push({ id: e.id, result: a.final ? 'final' : 'scored' });
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      console.error(`[score] entry ${e.id}:`, err);
      await fail(e.id, message);
      out.push({ id: e.id, result: 'failed', error: message });
    }
  }
  return out;
}
```

Create `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/entry-proof.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { privateKeyToAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
import { entryMessage, entrySigner } from './entry-proof';

const agent = privateKeyToAccount('0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d'); // anvil #1
const PAYOUT = '0xAbCdEf0123456789abcdef0123456789ABCDEF01';

test('entryMessage: the normalized repo and the lowercase payout wallet', () => {
  assert.equal(
    entryMessage('github.com/alice/tipjar', PAYOUT),
    'EarnKit entry: github.com/alice/tipjar 0xabcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01',
  );
});

test('entrySigner recovers the deploying wallet from any spelling of the repo link', async () => {
  const sig = await agent.signMessage({ message: entryMessage('github.com/alice/tipjar', PAYOUT) });
  const me = agent.address.toLowerCase();
  assert.equal(await entrySigner('https://github.com/Alice/TipJar.git', PAYOUT, sig), me);
  assert.equal(await entrySigner('github.com/alice/tipjar', PAYOUT.toLowerCase(), sig), me);
  // Another repo or another payout wallet recovers someone else.
  assert.notEqual(await entrySigner('https://github.com/alice/other', PAYOUT, sig), me);
  assert.notEqual(await entrySigner('https://github.com/alice/tipjar', '0x' + 'cd'.repeat(20), sig), me);
  assert.equal(await entrySigner('https://github.com/alice/tipjar', PAYOUT, '0x1234'), null);
});
```

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { pickWinners, rankEntries, standingText, type Candidate } from './winners';

const day = (d: number) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, d));
const e = (id: number, score: number | null, extra: Partial<Candidate> = {}): Candidate => ({
  id,
  score,
  submitted_at: day(id),
  payout_wallet: `0x${String(id).padStart(40, '0')}`,
  installation_id: 100 + id,
  vetoed_at: null,
  payout_status: null,
  prize_tx: null,
  ...extra,
});
const c = { winners: 2, min_score: 10 };

test('rankEntries: score, then the earlier entry; unscored entries have no rank', () => {
  const list = [e(3, 20), e(1, 20), e(2, null), e(4, 30)];
  assert.deepEqual(rankEntries(list).map((x) => x.id), [4, 1, 3]);
});

test('pickWinners: one prize per payout wallet and per installation; vetoes and refusals move the next entry up', () => {
  const W = '0x' + 'f'.repeat(40);
  const all = [
    e(1, 50, { payout_wallet: W }),
    e(2, 40, { payout_wallet: W.toUpperCase().replace('0X', '0x') }), // same wallet
    e(3, 35, { installation_id: 101 }), // same installation as #1
    e(4, 30, { vetoed_at: day(20) }),
    e(5, 25, { payout_status: 'refused' }),
    e(6, 20),
    e(7, 9), // below the minimum
  ];
  const r = pickWinners(all, 2, 10);
  assert.deepEqual(r.winners.map((x) => x.id), [1, 6]);
  assert.deepEqual(r.skipped, [
    { id: 2, reason: 'one prize per payout wallet: another entry with this wallet is ahead' },
    { id: 3, reason: "one prize per agent: this agent's other entry is ahead" },
    { id: 4, reason: 'removed in review' },
    { id: 5, reason: "the payout wallet can't receive prizes" },
  ]);
});

test('standingText while running: no rank until scored, top, behind, short of the minimum, ties', () => {
  const all = [e(1, 30), e(2, 14), e(3, 10), e(4, 4), e(5, null)];
  const run = { state: 'running' as const, ended: false };
  assert.equal(standingText(all[4], all, c, run), 'not scored yet');
  assert.equal(standingText(all[0], all, c, run), '#1, in the top 2');
  assert.equal(standingText(all[2], all, c, run), '#3, 4 users behind #2');
  assert.equal(standingText(all[3], all, c, run), '#4, 6 users short of the 10 minimum');
  const tie = [e(1, 30), e(2, 14), e(3, 14)];
  assert.equal(standingText(tie[2], tie, c, run), '#3, level with #2 (the earlier entry wins ties)');
  const mine = [e(1, 30, { installation_id: 7 }), e(2, 20, { installation_id: 7 }), e(3, 12)];
  assert.equal(standingText(mine[1], mine, c, run), "#2, one prize per agent: this agent's other entry is ahead");
});

test('standingText after the end: winner, in review, not in top N', () => {
  const all = [e(1, 30), e(2, 20), e(3, 15)];
  assert.equal(standingText(all[0], all, c, { state: 'review', ended: true }), 'in review');
  assert.equal(standingText(all[0], all, c, { state: 'paying', ended: true }), 'winner');
  assert.equal(standingText({ ...all[1], prize_tx: '0xabc' }, all, c, { state: 'paid', ended: true }), 'winner');
  assert.equal(standingText(all[2], all, c, { state: 'review', ended: true }), 'not in top 2');
  assert.equal(standingText(all[2], all, c, { state: 'running', ended: true }), 'not in top 2');
});
```

```ts
import { privateKeyToAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
import { campaignEntryProblems } from './projects';
import { EntryError } from './campaigns';
import { setSanctionsOracle } from './sanctions';
import { entryMessage } from './entry-proof';
import type { ProgramRow } from './programs';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const agentKey = privateKeyToAccount('0x5de4111afa1a4b94908f83103eb1f1706367c2e68ca870fc3fb9a804cdab365a'); // anvil #2
const PAY = '0x' + 'a'.repeat(40);
const C1 = '0x' + '1'.repeat(40);
const C2 = '0x' + '2'.repeat(40);
const campaignProgram = (): ProgramRow => {
  const f = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(import.meta.dirname, '../../../../programs/earnkit-campaign-1-base.json'), 'utf8'));
  return {
    ...f, status: 'active', starts_at: new Date('2026-10-05T00:00:00Z'), ends_at: new Date('2099-01-01T00:00:00Z'),
    verified_at: null, created_at: new Date(), opened_at: null, source: 'file', max_award_usd: '100',
    tag: null, token_policy: 'encouraged',
  } as ProgramRow;
};
const entryDeps = (deployer: Record<string, string>) => ({
  status: async () => 'during' as const,
  repoFacts: async () => ({ fork: false, created_at: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z', pushed_at: '2026-10-06T00:00:00Z' }),
  fetchFile: async () => ({ status: 'ok' as const, text: `payout_wallet: ${PAY}\n` }),
  deployer: { creationTx: async (c: string) => `0x${c.slice(2, 10)}`, txSender: async (h: string) => deployer[h] },
});
const input = (data: Record<string, unknown>, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  program_id: 'earnkit-campaign-1-base',
  project: { name: 'Tip Jar', repo_url: 'https://github.com/alice/tipjar', live_url: 'https://tipjar.example' },
  submission_data: {
    project_name: 'Tip Jar', description: 'Tips for creators', repo_url: 'https://github.com/alice/tipjar',
    live_url: 'https://tipjar.example', contracts: `${C1},${C2}`, payout_wallet: PAY, ...data,
  },
  ...extra,
});

test('v2.4 §2: every problem at once, not the first one', async () => {
  setSanctionsOracle({ chainId: async () => 8453, isSanctioned: async () => false });
  try {
    await assert.rejects(
      campaignEntryProblems(campaignProgram(), input({ description: '', contracts: `${C1},nope`, payout_wallet: '0xAbCdEf0123456789abcdef0123456789ABCDEF00' }) as never, 1, null, entryDeps({})),
      (err: unknown) => {
        assert.ok(err instanceof EntryError);
        assert.match(err.message, /^Fix these, then call record_submission again:\n- /);
        assert.equal(err.problems.length, 3);
        assert.match(err.problems[0], /Missing required answers: description/);
        assert.match(err.problems[1], /contracts: "nope" isn't an address/);
        assert.match(err.problems[2], /payout_wallet: .* wrong checksum/);
        return true;
      },
    );
  } finally {
    setSanctionsOracle(null);
  }
});

test("v2.4 §2: contracts deployed from the agent's own wallet enter with its signature", async () => {
  setSanctionsOracle({ chainId: async () => 8453, isSanctioned: async () => false });
  try {
    const agent = agentKey.address.toLowerCase();
    const deps = entryDeps({ [`0x${C1.slice(2, 10)}`]: agent, [`0x${C2.slice(2, 10)}`]: PAY });
    const signature = await agentKey.signMessage({ message: entryMessage('github.com/alice/tipjar', PAY) });
    const ok = await campaignEntryProblems(campaignProgram(), input({}, { deployer_signature: signature }) as never, 1, null, deps);
    assert.deepEqual(ok.entry, { contracts: [C1, C2], payoutWallet: PAY });
    assert.deepEqual(ok.deployers.sort(), [agent, PAY].sort());
    // Review Focus 2: a signature over the link form, or a checksummed wallet, isn't the entry message.
    const loose = await agentKey.signMessage({ message: `EarnKit entry: https://github.com/Alice/TipJar ${PAY.toUpperCase().replace('0X', '0x')}` });
    await assert.rejects(
      campaignEntryProblems(campaignProgram(), input({}, { deployer_signature: loose }) as never, 1, null, deps),
      (err: unknown) =>
        err instanceof EntryError &&
        err.problems.some((p) =>
          p.startsWith(`Contract ${C1} was deployed by ${agent}, not by the wallet that signed the entry`),
        ) &&
        err.problems.includes(
          `deployer_signature: sign exactly "EarnKit entry: github.com/alice/tipjar ${PAY}" with the wallet that deployed the contracts.`,
        ),
    );
  } finally {
    setSanctionsOracle(null);
  }
});
```

A signature over different text still recovers *some* address, just not the deployer, so recovery alone can't tell a typo from the wrong wallet. That is why `campaignEntryProblems` adds the "sign exactly …" hint whenever a signature was given and an ownership problem names a contract (Step 6).

- [ ] **Step 3: `entry-proof.ts`.**

```ts
import { recoverMessageAddress } from 'viem';
import { normalizeRepoUrl } from './campaigns';

/**
 * The text a deploying wallet signs (EIP-191 personal_sign) to enter its contracts for a payout
 * wallet (v2.4 §2): "EarnKit entry: <repo key> <payout wallet>", the repo key as normalizeRepoUrl
 * writes it (github.com/<owner>/<repo>, lowercase) and the wallet lowercase. Pure.
 */
export const entryMessage = (repoKey: string, payoutWallet: string) =>
  `EarnKit entry: ${repoKey} ${payoutWallet.toLowerCase()}`;

/** Who signed this repo's entry message for this payout wallet (lowercase), or null for a malformed signature. */
export async function entrySigner(repoUrl: string, payoutWallet: string, signature: string) {
  if (!/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{130}$/.test(signature)) return null;
  try {
    const signer = await recoverMessageAddress({
      message: entryMessage(normalizeRepoUrl(repoUrl), payoutWallet),
      signature: signature as `0x${string}`,
    });
    return signer.toLowerCase();
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `winners.ts`.**

```ts
/** What winner selection reads about an entry (projects row). */
export type Candidate = {
  id: number;
  score: number | null;
  submitted_at: Date;
  payout_wallet: string;
  installation_id: number | null;
  vetoed_at: Date | null;
  payout_status: string | null;
  prize_tx: string | null;
};

/** campaign_runs.state: the automatic lifecycle (v2.4 §3). */
export type RunState = 'running' | 'review' | 'paying' | 'paid';

/** Scored entries, best first: score, then the earlier entry (spec: ties go to it), then the id. Pure. */
export function rankEntries<T extends Candidate>(entries: T[]): T[] {
  return entries
    .filter((e) => e.score !== null)
    .sort(
      (a, b) =>
        b.score! - a.score! ||
        a.submitted_at.getTime() - b.submitted_at.getTime() ||
        a.id - b.id,
    );
}

/**
 * The winners in rank order (v2.4 §3): eligible (score >= minScore), not removed in review, payout
 * wallet not refused at payout, and one prize per payout wallet and per installation; a skipped
 * entry's slot goes to the next one. Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export function pickWinners<T extends Candidate>(entries: T[], winners: number, minScore: number) {
  const picked: T[] = [];
  const skipped: { id: number; reason: string }[] = [];
  const wallets = new Set<string>();
  const agents = new Set<number>();
  for (const e of rankEntries(entries)) {
    if (picked.length >= winners || e.score! < minScore) break;
    const wallet = e.payout_wallet.toLowerCase();
    const reason = e.vetoed_at
      ? 'removed in review'
      : e.payout_status === 'refused'
        ? "the payout wallet can't receive prizes"
        : wallets.has(wallet)
          ? 'one prize per payout wallet: another entry with this wallet is ahead'
          : e.installation_id !== null && agents.has(e.installation_id)
            ? "one prize per agent: this agent's other entry is ahead"
            : null;
    if (reason) {
      skipped.push({ id: e.id, reason });
      continue;
    }
    picked.push(e);
    wallets.add(wallet);
    if (e.installation_id !== null) agents.add(e.installation_id);
  }
  return { winners: picked, skipped };
}

/**
 * One entry's standing as builders read it (v2.4 §6 Standing). Unscored: "not scored yet" (no
 * rank). Running: "#3, in the top 5", "#7, 4 users behind #5", "#8, 6 users short of the 10
 * minimum", or "#4, <why it is skipped>". After the end: "winner" (paying, paid or paid out), "in
 * review", or "not in top 5". Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export function standingText(
  entry: Candidate,
  all: Candidate[],
  c: { winners: number; min_score: number },
  a: { state: RunState; ended: boolean },
) {
  if (entry.prize_tx) return 'winner';
  if (entry.score === null) return 'not scored yet';
  const picks = pickWinners(all, c.winners, c.min_score);
  const winner = picks.winners.some((w) => w.id === entry.id);
  if (a.ended || a.state !== 'running') {
    if (winner) return a.state === 'paying' || a.state === 'paid' ? 'winner' : 'in review';
    return `not in top ${c.winners}`;
  }
  const rank = rankEntries(all).findIndex((x) => x.id === entry.id) + 1;
  if (winner) return `#${rank}, in the top ${c.winners}`;
  const skipped = picks.skipped.find((s) => s.id === entry.id);
  if (skipped) return `#${rank}, ${skipped.reason}`;
  const short = c.min_score - entry.score;
  if (short > 0)
    return `#${rank}, ${short} user${short === 1 ? '' : 's'} short of the ${c.min_score} minimum`;
  const last = picks.winners.at(-1);
  if (!last || picks.winners.length < c.winners) return `#${rank}, in the top ${c.winners}`;
  const gap = last.score! - entry.score;
  return gap > 0
    ? `#${rank}, ${gap} user${gap === 1 ? '' : 's'} behind #${c.winners}`
    : `#${rank}, level with #${c.winners} (the earlier entry wins ties)`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `entry-text.ts`.**

```ts
import { payoutBy, prizeUsdEach, usd, type CampaignConfig } from './campaigns';
import { shortDate } from './dates';
import { TOKEN_TERMS } from './tokens';

/** EarnKit's public origin for links in builder text. */
export const siteUrl = () => (process.env.EARNKIT_URL ?? 'https://earnkit.com').replace(/\/$/, '');

/** "0xAbCd…Ef01". Pure. */
export const shortWallet = (w: string) => `${w.slice(0, 6)}…${w.slice(-4)}`;

const cell = (s: string) => s.replace(/\|/g, '\\|').replace(/\s*\n\s*/g, ' ');

/** A two-column markdown table whose first row is the header (it renders as a table). Pure; exported for tests. */
export function keyTable(rows: [string, string][]) {
  const [head, ...rest] = rows;
  return [
    `| ${cell(head[0])} | ${cell(head[1])} |`,
    '|---|---|',
    ...rest.map(([k, v]) => `| ${cell(k)} | ${cell(v)} |`),
  ].join('\n');
}

/** The one token row (v2.4: a default at submission only, never advertised). Pure. */
export const tokenRow = (symbol: string | null) =>
  symbol
    ? `$${symbol} on tokens.fun, you keep 40% of fees (say "no token" to skip)`
    : 'A token on tokens.fun, you keep 40% of fees: pick a name and ticker, or say "no token" to skip';

/** The line under the table: the earnings detail and both terms links (the v2.3 consent text). */
export const TOKEN_FOOTNOTE = `Token: 40% of trading fees after Clanker's 20% cut, paid in WETH; tokens.fun pays the gas. Terms: ${TOKEN_TERMS.agreement} · Risks: ${TOKEN_TERMS.risks}`;

/**
 * prepare_submission's show_to_builder (v2.4 §2): the one block the builder confirms. The prize
 * row is always first after the entry; the token row is one line; terms go under the table.
 * `prize` is the program's own prize text (programs that aren't EarnKit campaigns). Pure.
 */
export function confirmTable(a: {
  project: string;
  programTitle: string;
  campaign: CampaignConfig | null;
  prize: string;
  endsAt: Date | null;
  payoutWallet: string | null;
  token: { symbol: string | null } | null;
}) {
  const rows: [string, string][] = [['Entry', `${a.project} for ${a.programTitle}`]];
  const c = a.campaign;
  if (c?.kind === 'earnkit' && a.endsAt)
    rows.push([
      'Prize',
      `${usd(prizeUsdEach(c))} in ${c.prize.symbol} if top ${c.winners}, paid to ${a.payoutWallet ? shortWallet(a.payoutWallet) : 'your wallet'} on ${shortDate(payoutBy(a.endsAt))}`,
    ]);
  else if (c?.kind === 'sponsor' && a.endsAt)
    rows.push(['Prize', `the sponsor picks winners after ${shortDate(a.endsAt)}`]);
  else rows.push(['Prize', a.prize]);
  if (a.token) rows.push(['Token', tokenRow(a.token.symbol)]);
  return keyTable(rows) + (a.token ? `\n\n${TOKEN_FOOTNOTE}` : '');
}

/** record_submission's show_to_builder for a campaign entry (v2.4 §2): rank, leaderboard, payout date. Pure. */
export function enteredTable(a: {
  project: string;
  programTitle: string;
  standing: string;
  leaderboard: string;
  campaign: CampaignConfig;
  endsAt: Date | null;
  payoutWallet: string;
}) {
  const c = a.campaign;
  const ends = a.endsAt ?? new Date();
  return keyTable([
    ['Entered', `${a.project} in ${a.programTitle}`],
    ['Rank', a.standing],
    ['Leaderboard', a.leaderboard],
    [
      'Payout',
      c.kind === 'earnkit'
        ? `${usd(prizeUsdEach(c))} in ${c.prize.symbol} to ${shortWallet(a.payoutWallet)} on ${shortDate(payoutBy(ends))}, if top ${c.winners}`
        : `the sponsor picks winners after ${shortDate(ends)}; prizes go to ${shortWallet(a.payoutWallet)}`,
    ],
  ]);
}
```

`campaigns.ts`: replace `export class EntryError extends Error {}` with:

```ts
export class EntryError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    /** Every problem with the entry (v2.4 §2); one problem is its own message. */
    readonly problems: string[] = [message],
  ) {
    super(message);
  }

  /** All problems at once: one is its own message; several are a list. */
  static of(problems: string[]) {
    return new EntryError(
      problems.length === 1
        ? problems[0]
        : `Fix these, then call record_submission again:\n${problems.map((p) => `- ${p}`).join('\n')}`,
      problems,
    );
  }
}
```

Rename the body of `validateEntry` into `entryFieldProblems`. Keep the same checks in the same order, but push each message onto `problems` instead of throwing:
- contracts count;
- each malformed contract, as `contracts: "<bad>" isn't an address.`, one per bad one;
- the payout wallet format;
- the checksum;
- the payout wallet being one of the contracts.

A payout wallet that fails any of these three is returned as `payoutWallet: null`, so ownership and the sanctions screen are skipped for it. Only the first of the three that applies is reported.

Then, for each well-formed contract only, call `status(c)` in a `try`. Push the `not_contract` and `before_start` problems as before. A thrown read becomes `contracts: couldn't check ${c} on Base right now; try again in a minute.`. Return `{ contracts: wellFormed, payoutWallet: walletOk ? payout.toLowerCase() : null, problems }`, where `walletOk` is false once any payout-wallet check failed. Then:

```ts
/** validateEntry's checks, throwing the first problem (kept for callers that want one). */
export async function validateEntry(
  data: Record<string, unknown>,
  window: { starts_at: Date | null; ends_at: Date | null },
  opts: Parameters<typeof entryFieldProblems>[2] = {},
) {
  const r = await entryFieldProblems(data, window, opts);
  if (r.problems.length) throw new EntryError(r.problems[0]);
  return { contracts: r.contracts, payoutWallet: r.payoutWallet! };
}
```

The existing `'rejects bad entries with a clear reason'` test keeps passing, because the first problem is the same as before.

```ts
  const problems: string[] = [];
  for (const c of entry.contracts) {
    const d = deployers[c];
    if (d === undefined)
      problems.push(`contracts: EarnKit couldn't read who deployed ${c} from Base's explorer. Try again in a minute.`);
    else if (d !== entry.payoutWallet && !(entry.signer && d === entry.signer))
      problems.push(
        entry.signer
          ? `Contract ${c} was deployed by ${d ?? 'an unknown wallet'}, not by the wallet that signed the entry (${entry.signer}) or the payout wallet.`
          : `Contract ${c} was deployed by a different wallet. Enter contracts deployed from your payout wallet.`,
      );
  }
  return problems;
}

/** The first ownership problem, or null (kept for existing callers and tests). Pure. */
export const ownershipProblem = (...a: Parameters<typeof ownershipProblems>) =>
  ownershipProblems(...a)[0] ?? null;
```
