- Add the dependency type (import `OwnershipFetcher` from `./ownership`) and the problem collector:

```ts
/** Chain, explorer and GitHub reads, injectable for tests and the e2e. */
export type EntryDeps = {
  status?: (contract: string) => Promise<Deployment>;
  deployer?: DeployerLookup;
  repoFacts?: RepoFactsLookup;
  fetchFile?: OwnershipFetcher;
};

/**
 * Every check of a campaign entry, all problems at once (v2.4 §2): required answers, the repo
 * link, the contracts and payout wallet, the sponsor's own wallet, the deployer signature,
 * ownership (earnkit.txt and who deployed each contract) and the sanctions screen. Checks that
 * need an earlier answer (ownership needs a valid repo and wallet) run only once it is valid.
 * Throws EntryError.of(problems); otherwise returns the entry, its proof and deployers.
 */
export async function campaignEntryProblems(
  program: ProgramRow,
  input: z.infer<typeof RecordSubmissionInput>,
  installationId: number,
  sponsor: { installation_id: number; sponsor_wallet: string | null } | null,
  deps: EntryDeps = {},
) {
  const c = program.campaign!;
  const cleaned = cleanProject(input.project);
  const problems: string[] = [];
  const missing = missingAnswersProblem(program.submission_requirements, cleaned, input.submission_data);
  if (missing) problems.push(missing);
  let repoUrl: string | undefined;
  try {
    repoUrl = entryRepoUrl(cleaned.repo_url, input.submission_data);
    if (repoUrl) {
      const canon = canonicalBuilderUrl(repoUrl);
      if (canon.problem) {
        problems.push(`repo_url: ${canon.problem}.`);
        repoUrl = undefined;
      } else repoUrl = canon.url;
    } else if (!missing) problems.push('repo_url: campaign entries need the public GitHub repo of the project.');
  } catch (err) {
    if (!(err instanceof EntryError)) throw err;
    problems.push(err.message);
  }
  const fields = await entryFieldProblems(input.submission_data, program, {
    ...entryRules(c.kind, program.existing_work),
    status: deps.status,
  });
  problems.push(...fields.problems);
  const wallet = fields.payoutWallet;
  if (wallet) {
    const self = selfEntryProblem(sponsor, installationId, wallet);
    if (self) problems.push(self);
  }
  let signer: string | null = null;
  if (input.deployer_signature && repoUrl && wallet) {
    signer = await entrySigner(repoUrl, wallet, input.deployer_signature);
    if (!signer)
      problems.push('deployer_signature: it is not a valid signature (0x + 130 hex characters).');
  }
  let proof: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof checkOwnership>> | null = null;
  if (repoUrl && wallet) {
    try {
      proof = await checkOwnership(
        { repoUrl, payoutWallet: wallet, contracts: fields.contracts, signer },
        { deployer: deps.deployer, repoFacts: deps.repoFacts, ...(deps.fetchFile && { fetchFile: deps.fetchFile }) },
      );
    } catch (err) {
      if (!(err instanceof EntryError)) throw err;
      problems.push(...err.problems);
      // Review Focus 2: a signature over other text recovers another wallet; say exactly what to sign.
      if (input.deployer_signature && err.problems.some((p) => p.startsWith('Contract ')))
        problems.push(
          `deployer_signature: sign exactly "${entryMessage(normalizeRepoUrl(repoUrl), wallet)}" with the wallet that deployed the contracts.`,
        );
    }
  }
  if (wallet) {
    try {
      await screenWallet(
        wallet,
        c.kind === 'sponsor' ? c.prize.chain_id : BASE_MAINNET,
        `payout_wallet: ${CANT_RECEIVE}`,
      );
    } catch (err) {
      if (!(err instanceof SanctionsError)) throw err;
      problems.push(err.message);
    }
  }
  if (problems.length) throw EntryError.of(problems);
  return {
    repoUrl: repoUrl!,
    entry: { contracts: fields.contracts, payoutWallet: wallet! },
    proof: proof!,
    deployers: [...new Set(Object.values(proof!.deployers).filter(Boolean))],
  };
}
```

Imports to add in `projects.ts`: `entryFieldProblems` from `./campaigns`; `entrySigner` and `entryMessage` from `./entry-proof`; `SanctionsError` from `./sanctions`; `type OwnershipFetcher` from `./ownership`. Remove the `validateEntry` import if it is no longer used.

The problem for a malformed `checkOwnership` `deps` override: `checkOwnership`'s second parameter destructures with defaults, so passing `fetchFile: undefined` would override the default. That is why the spread above adds `fetchFile` only when it is set.

```ts
    entry = checked.entry;
    proof = checked.proof;
    deployers = checked.deployers;
```

```ts
test('Fastest: prize per hour of effort; programs needing videos or forms never go first', () => {

  const text = presentFirstMessage(msg({
    campaigns: [{ ...campaign, you: '#3, in the top 5' }],
    cards: [base, arc],
    repos: repos(v('base-builder-grants', 'likely', 'looks eligible', 'none'), v('arc-microgrants', 'after_step', 'needs a live link', 'minutes')),
  }));
  assert.match(text, /Want me to start on Arc Microgrants with tipjar\?$/);
  // Only forms programs fit: no "start on", the generic offer.
  const only = presentFirstMessage(msg({ cards: [base], repos: repos(v('base-builder-grants', 'likely', 'looks eligible')) }));
  assert.match(only, /Pick one and I'll start\.$/);
});

test('one offer: keep going beats the campaign; a sponsor campaign is never offered or in the repo table', () => {
  const resume = presentFirstMessage(msg({ campaigns: [campaign], cards: [arc], workingOn: ['arc-microgrants'] }));
  assert.match(resume, /Want me to keep going on Arc Microgrants\?$/);
  const sponsorOnly = presentFirstMessage(msg({ cards: [sponsored], repos: repos(v('sponsor-abc', 'likely', 'looks eligible')) }));
  assert.doesNotMatch(sponsorOnly, /Your repos/);
  assert.match(sponsorOnly, /None of your 1 repos fits an open program yet\./);
  assert.match(sponsorOnly, /Pick one and I'll start\.$/);
});

  const cards: Card[] = [
    { ...arc, is_new: true },
    { ...tiny, closes_in_days: 1 },
    { ...sponsored, is_new: true },
  ];
  assert.deepEqual(programAlerts(cards, false), ['new: Arc Microgrants', '1 new sponsored campaign', 'Tiny Bounty closes in 1 day']);
  assert.deepEqual(programAlerts(cards, true), []);
});

test('closesInDays: within 3 days only, never negative', () => {
  const now = new Date('2026-10-04T00:00:00Z');
  assert.equal(closesInDays(new Date('2026-10-06T12:00:00Z'), now), 2);
  assert.equal(closesInDays(new Date('2026-10-20T00:00:00Z'), now), null);
  assert.equal(closesInDays(new Date('2026-10-03T00:00:00Z'), now), 0);
  assert.equal(closesInDays(null, now), null);
});
```

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { campaignRow } from './leaderboard';
import type { CampaignConfig } from './campaigns';

const campaign: CampaignConfig = {
  kind: 'earnkit',
  prize: { token: '0x' + 'a'.repeat(40), symbol: 'MINI', decimals: 18, chain: 'base' },
  winners: 5, amount_each: '1', prize_usd_total: 500, metric: 'unique_wallets', min_score: 10,
};
const now = new Date('2026-10-04T12:00:00Z');
const p = { id: 'c1', title: 'C1', campaign, ends_at: new Date('2026-10-18T23:59:00Z'), status: 'active' as const, state: null };
const entry = (id: number, score: number | null) => ({
  id, program_id: 'c1', score, submitted_at: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 1, id)), payout_wallet: `0x${String(id).padStart(40, '0')}`,
  installation_id: id, vetoed_at: null, payout_status: null, prize_tx: null,
});

test('campaignRow: live numbers without an entry; the builder\'s best standing with one', () => {
  const entries = [30, 25, 20, 18, 14, 9, null].map((s, i) => entry(i + 1, s));
  const row = campaignRow(p, entries, new Set(), now);
  assert.deepEqual(
    { prize: row.prize, entries: row.entries, cutoff: row.cutoff, you: row.you, open: row.open, each: row.prize_usd_each },
    { prize: '$100 each to top 5 (in MINI)', entries: 7, cutoff: 14, you: null, open: true, each: 100 },
  );
  assert.equal(campaignRow(p, entries, new Set([6, 7]), now).you, '#6, 1 user short of the 10 minimum');
  assert.equal(campaignRow(p, entries, new Set([3, 7]), now).you, '#3, in the top 5');
  assert.equal(campaignRow(p, entries, new Set([7]), now).you, 'not scored yet');
  const ended = campaignRow({ ...p, ends_at: new Date('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z'), state: 'review' }, entries, new Set([2]), now);
  assert.deepEqual([ended.open, ended.you], [false, 'in review']);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite `present.ts`.** Keep `closesInDays`, `soon` and `YourRepos` as they are. Remove `presentSinceLastCheck`, `presentPrograms`, `line`, `rank`, `MAX_REPOS` and `byFit`. The new file:

```ts
import { usd } from './campaigns';
import { shortDate } from './dates';
import { EFFORT_ORDER, type Effort, type Verdict } from './match';

/**
 * The builder-facing text for list_programs (v2.4 §1): money first, markdown tables, no emojis.
 * Built on the server because many MCP clients never show the model the server instructions; a
 * ready-made result gets shown as written. Order, always: "Since you last checked", EarnKit's own
 * campaigns, what the builder's repos qualify for, other open programs (at most 5), sponsor
 * campaigns (never first), then one offer.
 */
export type Card = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  summary: string;
  tag: string | null;
  each_time: string[];
  ends_at?: Date | null;
  /** Opened since the builder's last check. */
  is_new?: boolean;
  /** Whole days until the deadline, when it's 3 or fewer. */
  closes_in_days?: number | null;
  /** The known per-winner award in USD (programs.ts perWinnerUsd); unknown sorts last. */
  max_award_usd?: number | null;
  /**
   * A sponsor campaign (verified or not): its own section after curated programs, never in a
   * repo's table, the closing offer or by name in the alerts (re-audit H4, M6).
   */
  sponsored?: boolean;
  /** Required answers only the builder can give (personal info, forms). */
  builder_fields?: number;
};

/** One EarnKit campaign row (leaderboard.ts campaignRow). */
export type CampaignRow = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  /** "$100 each to top 5 (in MINI)". */
  prize: string;
  prize_usd_each: number;
  ends_at: Date;
  entries: number;
  /** The score at #winners (rank order), or null while fewer entries are scored. */
  cutoff: number | null;
  winners: number;
  /** The builder's standing (winners.ts standingText), or null without an entry. */
  you: string | null;
  open: boolean;
};

export type FirstMessage = {
  /** "Since you last checked" items, money first (alerts.ts, then programAlerts). */
  alerts: string[];
  campaigns: CampaignRow[];
  /** Open programs that aren't EarnKit campaigns (curated and sponsored). */
  cards: Card[];
  repos: YourRepos | null;
  workingOn: string[];
  agentCanBuild: boolean;
  /** "Your campaign entries" for sponsor campaigns (campaigns.ts presentYourEntries), or null. */
  sponsorEntries: string | null;
  now: Date;
};

const DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
/** Rows in "Also open" (spec §1). */
const MAX_OTHERS = 5;

// closesInDays, soon and YourRepos: unchanged from v2.3.

/**
 * "Since you last checked" items about programs: new curated ones by name, new sponsored ones
 * counted (re-audit H4), curated ones closing within 3 days. None on a first check. Pure.
 */
export function programAlerts(cards: Card[], firstCheck: boolean): string[] {
  if (firstCheck) return [];
  const named = cards.filter((c) => !c.sponsored);
  const sponsoredNew = cards.filter((c) => c.sponsored && c.is_new).length;
  return [
    ...named.filter((c) => c.is_new).map((c) => `new: ${c.title}`),
    ...(sponsoredNew > 0
      ? [`${sponsoredNew} new sponsored campaign${sponsoredNew === 1 ? '' : 's'}`]
      : []),
    ...named
      .filter((c) => c.closes_in_days != null)
      .map((c) => `${c.title} ${soon(c.closes_in_days!)}`),
  ];
}

/** A markdown table; "|" is escaped and line breaks folded so a cell can't break it. Pure; exported for tests. */
export function table(head: string[], rows: string[][]) {
  const cell = (s: string) => s.replace(/\|/g, '\\|').replace(/\s*\n\s*/g, ' ');
  return [
    `| ${head.map(cell).join(' | ')} |`,
    `|${head.map(() => '---').join('|')}|`,
    ...rows.map((r) => `| ${r.map(cell).join(' | ')} |`),
  ].join('\n');
}

/** The prize part of a program's summary ("500 USDC × 20 grants"). */
const prizeOf = (c: Card) => c.summary.split(' · ')[0];

/** "Oct 14" from the summary's "closes Oct 14, …", "rolling", else ends_at's UTC day. Pure; exported for tests. */
export function endsOf(c: Card) {
  for (const part of c.summary.split(' · ').slice(1)) {
    const m = /^closes (\w{3} \d{1,2})\b/.exec(part);
    if (m) return m[1];
    if (part === 'rolling') return 'rolling';
  }
  return c.ends_at ? shortDate(c.ends_at) : 'rolling';
}

const left = (endsAt: Date, now: Date) => {
  const days = Math.floor((endsAt.getTime() - now.getTime()) / DAY_MS);
  return days <= 0 ? 'ends today' : `${days} day${days === 1 ? '' : 's'} left`;
};

/** A campaign's "You" cell without an entry: "12 entered, #5 has 14 users". Pure; exported for tests. */
export function fieldText(r: CampaignRow) {
  if (r.entries === 0) return 'no entries yet';
  return r.cutoff === null
    ? `${r.entries} entered, top ${r.winners} not full`
    : `${r.entries} entered, #${r.winners} has ${r.cutoff} users`;
}

/** Hours of builder effort per verdict effort, for "fastest" (v2.4 §6). */
const EFFORT_HOURS: Record<Effort, number> = { none: 0.5, minutes: 0.5, hours: 4, days: 24, weeks: 120 };

/** Programs needing videos or forms (each-time items, or more than 2 answers only the builder can give) never go first. Pure. */
export const needsForms = (c: Card) => c.each_time.length > 0 || (c.builder_fields ?? 0) > 2;

/** Known prize per hour of builder effort. Pure; exported for tests. */
export const prizePerHour = (c: Card, v: Verdict) => (c.max_award_usd ?? 0) / EFFORT_HOURS[v.effort];

const quickness = (v: Verdict) => (v.status === 'likely' ? 0 : 10) + EFFORT_ORDER.indexOf(v.effort);

/** Larger known per-winner prize first (unknown last), then the earlier deadline (rolling last). */
const byPrize = (a: Card, b: Card) =>
  (b.max_award_usd ?? -1) - (a.max_award_usd ?? -1) ||
  (a.ends_at?.getTime() ?? 8.64e15) - (b.ends_at?.getTime() ?? 8.64e15);

const CHAT_ONLY =
  "I can't build or deploy from here, but I can plan it and write every answer for you to submit. To have it done for you, add EarnKit to a coding agent (Claude Code, Cursor, Codex).";

/** The first message: see the type and the module comment for the order. Pure; exported for tests. */
export function presentFirstMessage(m: FirstMessage): string {
  const sections: string[] = [];
  if (m.alerts.length) sections.push(`Since you last checked: ${m.alerts.join('; ')}.`);
  if (m.campaigns.length === 0 && m.cards.length === 0)
    return [...sections, 'No open programs right now. Check back soon.'].join('\n\n');

  if (m.campaigns.length > 0)
    sections.push(
      table(
        ['Win', 'Prize', 'Ends', 'You'],
        m.campaigns.map((r) => [
          r.title,
          r.prize,
          r.open ? `${shortDate(r.ends_at)}, ${left(r.ends_at, m.now)}` : `ended ${shortDate(r.ends_at)}`,
          r.you ?? fieldText(r),
        ]),
      ),
    );

  // What the builder's repos qualify for: each curated program once, under the repo that fits it
  // best, sorted by prize per winner.
  const byId = new Map(m.cards.map((c) => [c.id, c]));
  const curated = (id: string) => byId.has(id) && !byId.get(id)!.sponsored;
  const fits = m.repos && 'fits' in m.repos ? m.repos.fits : [];
  const matched = new Map<string, { card: Card; repo: string; v: Verdict }>();
  for (const f of fits)
    for (const v of [...f.verdicts].sort((a, b) => quickness(a) - quickness(b)))
      if (v.status !== 'no' && curated(v.program_id) && !matched.has(v.program_id))
        matched.set(v.program_id, { card: byId.get(v.program_id)!, repo: f.repo, v });
  const rows = [...matched.values()].sort((a, b) => byPrize(a.card, b.card));
  if (m.repos && 'error' in m.repos) sections.push(`Couldn't read your repos: ${m.repos.error}`);
  else if (m.repos && rows.length === 0)
    sections.push(`None of your ${m.repos.checked} repos fits an open program yet.`);
  else if (rows.length > 0)
    sections.push(
      `${rows.every((r) => r.v.status === 'likely') ? 'Your repos already qualify for:' : 'Your repos can enter:'}\n\n` +
        table(
          ['Program', 'Prize', 'Ends', 'Repo'],
          rows.map(({ card, repo, v }) => [
            card.title,
            prizeOf(card),
            endsOf(card),
            v.status === 'likely' || !v.note ? repo : `${repo}, ${v.note}`,
          ]),
        ),
    );

  const rest = m.cards.filter((c) => !c.sponsored && !matched.has(c.id)).sort(byPrize);
  if (rest.length > 0) {
    const shown = rest.slice(0, MAX_OTHERS);
    const more = rest.length - shown.length;
    const above = m.campaigns.length > 0 || rows.length > 0;
    sections.push(
      `${above ? 'Also open:' : 'Open now:'}\n\n` +
        table(['Program', 'Prize', 'Ends'], shown.map((c) => [c.title, prizeOf(c), endsOf(c)])) +
        (more > 0 ? `\n\n${more} more open: ask me for the full list.` : ''),
    );
  }

  const sponsored = m.cards.filter((c) => c.sponsored);
  if (sponsored.length > 0)
    sections.push(
      "Sponsored (set by sponsors; EarnKit didn't write or check them):\n\n" +
        table(['Campaign', 'Details'], sponsored.map((c) => [c.title, c.summary])),
    );
  if (m.sponsorEntries) sections.push(m.sponsorEntries);

  sections.push(offer(m, byId, rows));
  return sections.join('\n\n');
}

/** One closing offer, never a menu and never a sponsor campaign. */
function offer(
  m: FirstMessage,
  byId: Map<string, Card>,
  rows: { card: Card; repo: string; v: Verdict }[],
) {
  if (!m.agentCanBuild) return CHAT_ONLY;
  const titleOf = (id: string) => {
    const c = byId.get(id);
    if (c) return c.sponsored ? undefined : c.title;
    return m.campaigns.find((r) => r.id === id && r.open)?.title;
  };
  const resume = m.workingOn.map(titleOf).find(Boolean);
  if (resume) return `Want me to keep going on ${resume}?`;
  const campaign = m.campaigns.find((r) => r.open && r.you === null);
  if (campaign) return `Want me to build your entry for the ${usd(campaign.prize_usd_each)}?`;
  const fastest = rows
    .filter((r) => !needsForms(r.card))
    .sort((a, b) => prizePerHour(b.card, b.v) - prizePerHour(a.card, a.v) || quickness(a.v) - quickness(b.v))[0];
  if (fastest) return `Want me to start on ${fastest.card.title} with ${fastest.repo}?`;
  return "Pick one and I'll start.";
}
```


- [ ] **Step 5: Campaign rows.** In `leaderboard.ts`, add (imports: `pickWinners`, `rankEntries`, `standingText`, `type Candidate`, `type RunState` from `./winners`; `prizeColumn`, `prizeUsdEach`, `type EarnKitCampaign` from `./campaigns`; `type CampaignRow` from `./present`):

```ts
/** Every entry of these campaigns as winner selection reads it. */
export async function campaignCandidates(programIds: string[]) {
  if (programIds.length === 0) return [];
  return sql()<(Candidate & { program_id: string })[]>`
    SELECT id, program_id, score, submitted_at, payout_wallet, installation_id, vetoed_at,
      payout_status, prize_tx
    FROM projects
    WHERE program_id IN ${sql()(programIds)} AND payout_wallet IS NOT NULL`;
}

/** One EarnKit campaign row for list_programs (v2.4 §1): live numbers and the builder's best standing. Pure; exported for tests. */
export function campaignRow(
  p: { id: string; title: string; campaign: CampaignConfig; ends_at: Date; status: 'active' | 'closed'; state: RunState | null },
  entries: Candidate[],
  mine: Set<number>,
  now: Date,
): CampaignRow {
  const c = p.campaign as EarnKitCampaign;
  const ranked = rankEntries(entries);
  const ended = p.ends_at <= now;
  const state = p.state ?? 'running';
  const own = entries.filter((e) => mine.has(e.id));
  // The builder's best entry: the best-ranked scored one, else any.
  const best = rankEntries(own)[0] ?? own[0];
  return {
    id: p.id,
    title: p.title,
    prize: prizeColumn(c),
    prize_usd_each: prizeUsdEach(c),
    ends_at: p.ends_at,
    entries: entries.length,
    cutoff: ranked.length >= c.winners ? ranked[c.winners - 1].score : null,
    winners: c.winners,
    you: best ? standingText(best, entries, c, { state, ended }) : null,
    open: !ended && p.status === 'active',
  };
}

/**
 * EarnKit campaigns for the first message: open ones, plus ended ones not yet paid out where this
 * installation (or one of its wallets) has an entry (v2.4 §1, §5).
 */
export async function earnkitCampaignRows(installationId: number, now = new Date()) {
  const db = sql();
  const mineSql = () => db`(x.installation_id = ${installationId}
    OR x.payout_wallet IN (SELECT wallet FROM installation_wallets WHERE installation_id = ${installationId}))`;
  const programs = await db<
    { id: string; title: string; campaign: CampaignConfig; ends_at: Date; status: 'active' | 'closed'; state: RunState | null }[]
  >`
    SELECT p.id, p.title, p.campaign, p.ends_at, p.status, r.state
    FROM programs p LEFT JOIN campaign_runs r ON r.program_id = p.id
    WHERE p.campaign->>'kind' = 'earnkit' AND p.ends_at IS NOT NULL AND (
      (p.status = 'active' AND p.ends_at > ${now})
      OR (COALESCE(r.state, 'running') <> 'paid' AND p.ends_at <= ${now}
          AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM projects x WHERE x.program_id = p.id AND ${mineSql()})))
    ORDER BY p.ends_at`;
  if (programs.length === 0) return [];
  const entries = await campaignCandidates(programs.map((p) => p.id));
  const mine = new Set(
    (await db<{ id: number }[]>`
      SELECT x.id FROM projects x WHERE x.program_id IN ${db(programs.map((p) => p.id))} AND ${mineSql()}`).map((r) => r.id),
  );
  return programs.map((p) =>
    campaignRow(p, entries.filter((e) => e.program_id === p.id), mine, now),
  );
}
```

### Task 6: Alerts engine

Covers spec §4:
- "Since you last checked" opens every tool reply: new campaigns by `opened_at`, rank changes into or out of the top 5, prizes paid with the transaction link, and token fees of $100 or more (FeeLocker read, 6 h cache).

It also fixes audit C11 and C12.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/fees.ts`, `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/alerts.ts`
- Modify: `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/tools.ts` (every builder tool reply; `list_programs` input `wallet`; the schedule note replaces `ALERTS_OFFER`)

- Test: Create `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/fees.test.ts`, `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/alerts.test.ts`; Modify `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/mcp.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `installation_wallets`, `installations.alerts_checked_at | alert_state | fee_alerted_usd | schedule_offered_at`, `token_fees`, `projects.creator_wallet` (Task 1)
  - `llamaPrice` (Task 2)
  - `pickWinners`, `campaignCandidates`, `wonUsdText`, `usd`, `prizeColumn`
  - `rememberWallets` (Task 1)
- Produces:
  - `FEE_LOCKER`, `WETH_BASE`, `FEE_ALERT_USD = 100`, `FEE_TTL_MS`
  - `type FeeSource = { availableWeth(wallet: string): Promise<bigint>; wethUsd(): Promise<number> }`, `setFeeSource(s: FeeSource | null)`
  - `wethToUsd(wei: bigint, price: number): number`
  - `feeAlert(usdNow: number, lastShown: number | null): { show: boolean; next: number | null }`
  - `refreshFees(wallets: string[]): Promise<number>`, `cachedFeesUsd(wallets: string[]): Promise<number | null>`
  - `launchedCreatorWallets(): Promise<string[]>` (fees.ts)
  - `type AlertFacts`, `type AlertMemory`
  - `alertLines(prev: AlertMemory, f: AlertFacts): { lines: string[]; state: AlertState; feeAlertedUsd: number | null }`
  - `alertFacts(installationId: number): Promise<AlertFacts>`
  - `sinceYouLastChecked(installationId: number): Promise<{ lines: string[]; scheduleNote: string | null }>`
  - `sinceLine(lines: string[]): string | null`
  - `SCHEDULE_NOTE` (alerts.ts)
  - Tool replies gain `since_you_last_checked` (first key) and, once per installation, `schedule_daily_check`.

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { alertLines, sinceLine, type AlertFacts } from './alerts';

const empty: AlertFacts = { newCampaigns: [], standings: [], prizes: [], fees: null };
const t = (h: number) => new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 10, h));

test('a prize paid since the last check, with its amount and transaction link; not again', () => {
  const f: AlertFacts = { ...empty, prizes: [{ project_id: 1, name: 'Tip Jar', won: '$100 in MINI', prize_tx: '0xabc', paid_at: t(5) }] };
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: t(4), state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, f).lines, [
    'you won $100 in MINI with Tip Jar (basescan.org/tx/0xabc)',
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: t(6), state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, f).lines, []);
});

test('rank changes into and out of the top N, only for scored entries and only against what was seen', () => {
  const s = (inTop: boolean, scored = true) => ({ project_id: 7, name: 'Tip Jar', campaign_title: 'C1', winners: 5, scored, inTop });
  const prev = { checkedAt: t(1), feeAlertedUsd: null };
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ ...prev, state: { top: { 7: false } } }, { ...empty, standings: [s(true)] }).lines, ['Tip Jar moved into the top 5 of C1']);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ ...prev, state: { top: { 7: true } } }, { ...empty, standings: [s(false)] }).lines, ['Tip Jar dropped out of the top 5 of C1']);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ ...prev, state: { top: { 7: true } } }, { ...empty, standings: [s(true)] }).lines, []);
  // First score straight into the top: news. First-ever check (no state): silent.
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ ...prev, state: { top: {} } }, { ...empty, standings: [s(true)] }).lines, ['Tip Jar moved into the top 5 of C1']);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: null, state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, { ...empty, standings: [s(true)] }).lines, []);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ ...prev, state: { top: {} } }, { ...empty, standings: [s(true, false)] }).state, { top: {} });
});

test('a campaign opened since the last check is news (opened_at, not created_at)', () => {
  const f: AlertFacts = { ...empty, newCampaigns: [{ id: 'c1', title: 'EarnKit Campaign #1', prize: '$100 each to top 5 (in MINI)', opened_at: t(5) }] };
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: t(4), state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, f).lines, ['new: EarnKit Campaign #1 ($100 each to top 5 (in MINI))']);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: t(6), state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, f).lines, []);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: null, state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, f).lines, []);
});

test('token fees from $100, once per crossing; money lines first', () => {
  const f: AlertFacts = {
    ...empty,
    fees: { usd: 138, tokens: [{ url: 'https://tokens.fun/coin/0xabc', symbol: 'TIP' }] },
    prizes: [{ project_id: 1, name: 'Tip Jar', won: '$100 in MINI', prize_tx: '0x1', paid_at: t(5) }],
    newCampaigns: [{ id: 'c2', title: 'C2', prize: '$50 each to top 3 (in MINI)', opened_at: t(5) }],
  };

  assert.deepEqual(r.lines, [
    'you won $100 in MINI with Tip Jar (basescan.org/tx/0x1)',
    'your token $TIP has $138 in fees to claim (tokens.fun/coin/0xabc)',
    'new: C2 ($50 each to top 3 (in MINI))',
  ]);
  assert.equal(r.feeAlertedUsd, 138);
  assert.deepEqual(alertLines({ checkedAt: t(6), state: null, feeAlertedUsd: 138 }, f).lines, []);

  assert.match(alertLines({ checkedAt: t(6), state: null, feeAlertedUsd: null }, two).lines[0], /^your tokens have \$250 in fees to claim \(tokens\.fun\/coin\/0xa\)$/);
});

test('sinceLine', () => {

  assert.equal(sinceLine(['a', 'b']), 'Since you last checked: a; b.');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `fees.ts`.**

```ts
import { createPublicClient, http } from 'viem';
import { base } from 'viem/chains';
import { sql } from './db';
import { llamaPrice } from './prices';

/** Clanker's FeeLocker on Base: creator fees wait here until claimed (v2.4 §4). */
export const FEE_LOCKER = '0xf3622742b1e446d92e45e22923ef11c2fcd55d68';
export const WETH_BASE = '0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006';
/** Fee alerts only from this much (spec: "above $100", to keep reads and noise down). */
export const FEE_ALERT_USD = 100;
export const FEE_TTL_MS = 6 * 3_600_000;

const feeLockerAbi = [
  {
    type: 'function',
    name: 'availableFees',
    stateMutability: 'view',
    inputs: [
      { name: 'feeOwner', type: 'address' },
      { name: 'token', type: 'address' },
    ],
    outputs: [{ type: 'uint256' }],
  },
] as const;

/** Where fee balances come from; replaced in tests and the e2e. */
export type FeeSource = {
  /** WETH (wei) waiting for `wallet` in the FeeLocker: fees already there, nothing else. */
  availableWeth: (wallet: string) => Promise<bigint>;
  wethUsd: () => Promise<number>;
};

const chain: FeeSource = {
  availableWeth: (wallet) =>
    createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(process.env.BASE_RPC_URL) }).readContract({
      address: FEE_LOCKER,
      abi: feeLockerAbi,
      functionName: 'availableFees',
      args: [wallet as `0x${string}`, WETH_BASE],
    }),
  wethUsd: async () => (await llamaPrice(`base:${WETH_BASE}`)).price,
};
let source: FeeSource = chain;
/** Tests and the e2e: replace the source (null restores the chain). */
export const setFeeSource = (s: FeeSource | null) => {
  source = s ?? chain;
};

/** WETH wei at a USD price. Pure; exported for tests. */
export const wethToUsd = (wei: bigint, price: number) => (Number(wei) / 1e18) * price;

/**
 * Whether to show fees now (v2.4 §4): from FEE_ALERT_USD, once per crossing, and again each time
 * they grow by another FEE_ALERT_USD; below it (claimed) the memory resets. Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export function feeAlert(usdNow: number, lastShown: number | null) {
  if (usdNow < FEE_ALERT_USD) return { show: false, next: null };
  if (lastShown === null || usdNow >= lastShown + FEE_ALERT_USD) return { show: true, next: usdNow };
  return { show: false, next: lastShown };
}

/** Reads and caches the fees waiting for these creator wallets; returns how many were read. */
export async function refreshFees(wallets: string[]) {
  const list = [...new Set(wallets.map((w) => w.toLowerCase()))];
  if (list.length === 0) return 0;
  const price = await source.wethUsd();
  for (const w of list) {
    const weth = await source.availableWeth(w);
    await sql()`
      INSERT INTO token_fees (wallet, weth, usd, read_at)
      VALUES (${w}, ${weth.toString()}, ${wethToUsd(weth, price)}, now())
      ON CONFLICT (wallet) DO UPDATE SET weth = EXCLUDED.weth, usd = EXCLUDED.usd, read_at = EXCLUDED.read_at`;
  }
  return list.length;
}

const inflight = new Set<string>();

/**
 * The cached fees (USD) waiting for these wallets, summed; null when none was ever read. Never
 * waits on a read: missing or stale (over 6 h) wallets are refreshed in the background, at most 4
 * at a time, so the next reply has them.
 */
export async function cachedFeesUsd(wallets: string[], now = Date.now()) {
  const list = [...new Set(wallets.map((w) => w.toLowerCase()))];
  if (list.length === 0) return null;
  const rows = await sql()<{ wallet: string; usd: string; read_at: Date }[]>`
    SELECT wallet, usd, read_at FROM token_fees WHERE wallet IN ${sql()(list)}`;
  const stale = list.filter((w) => {
    const r = rows.find((x) => x.wallet === w);
    return !r || now - r.read_at.getTime() > FEE_TTL_MS;
  });
  const todo = stale.filter((w) => !inflight.has(w)).slice(0, Math.max(0, 4 - inflight.size));
  if (todo.length > 0) {
    todo.forEach((w) => inflight.add(w));
    void refreshFees(todo)
      .catch((err) => console.error('[fees]', err))
      .finally(() => todo.forEach((w) => inflight.delete(w)));
  }
  return rows.length ? rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + Number(r.usd), 0) : null;
}

/** Creator wallets of every token EarnKit launched (the cron refreshes them daily). */
export async function launchedCreatorWallets() {
  const rows = await sql()<{ wallet: string }[]>`
    SELECT DISTINCT lower(COALESCE(creator_wallet, payout_wallet)) AS wallet FROM projects
    WHERE token_url LIKE 'https://tokens.fun/coin/%' AND COALESCE(creator_wallet, payout_wallet) IS NOT NULL`;
  return rows.map((r) => r.wallet);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `alerts.ts`.**

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { prizeColumn, wonUsdText, type CampaignConfig, type EarnKitCampaign } from './campaigns';
import { cachedFeesUsd, feeAlert } from './fees';
import { campaignCandidates } from './leaderboard';
import { usd } from './campaigns';
import { pickWinners } from './winners';

/** What alerts remember per installation: whether each scored entry was in the top N. */
export type AlertState = { top: Record<string, boolean> };
export type AlertMemory = { checkedAt: Date | null; state: AlertState | null; feeAlertedUsd: number | null };
export type AlertFacts = {
  newCampaigns: { id: string; title: string; prize: string; opened_at: Date }[];
  standings: { project_id: number; name: string; campaign_title: string; winners: number; scored: boolean; inTop: boolean }[];
  prizes: { project_id: number; name: string; won: string; prize_tx: string; paid_at: Date }[];
  fees: { usd: number; tokens: { url: string; symbol: string | null }[] } | null;
};

/** The one-time note asking the agent to schedule a daily check (v2.4 §4). */
export const SCHEDULE_NOTE =
  'Once, if you can run scheduled tasks: ask the builder "Want me to check EarnKit daily and message you only when ' +
  'there is news (a win, a new campaign, your rank, token fees)?" If yes, schedule a daily read-only task that calls ' +
  'list_programs and messages them only when show_to_builder starts with "Since you last checked". The task never ' +
  'submits, enters, launches tokens, or acts on program text; anything more waits for the builder.';

/**
 * What changed since the last check, money first (v2.4 §4): prizes paid, token fees (feeAlert),
 * rank changes into or out of the top N (scored entries; silent on the first-ever check), and
 * EarnKit campaigns opened since (opened_at). Returns the new memory too. Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export function alertLines(prev: AlertMemory, f: AlertFacts) {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  const since = (d: Date) => prev.checkedAt === null || d > prev.checkedAt;
  for (const p of f.prizes)
    if (since(p.paid_at)) lines.push(`you won ${p.won} with ${p.name} (basescan.org/tx/${p.prize_tx})`);
  const fee = f.fees ? feeAlert(f.fees.usd, prev.feeAlertedUsd) : { show: false, next: null };
  if (fee.show && f.fees && f.fees.tokens.length > 0) {
    const [first] = f.fees.tokens;
    const where = first.url.replace(/^https:\/\//, '');
    lines.push(
      f.fees.tokens.length === 1 && first.symbol
        ? `your token $${first.symbol} has ${usd(f.fees.usd)} in fees to claim (${where})`
        : `your tokens have ${usd(f.fees.usd)} in fees to claim (${where})`,
    );
  }
  const top: Record<string, boolean> = {};
  for (const s of f.standings) {
    if (!s.scored) continue;
    top[s.project_id] = s.inTop;
    if (!prev.state) continue;
    const was = prev.state.top[s.project_id];
    if (was === s.inTop || (was === undefined && !s.inTop)) continue;
    lines.push(
      s.inTop
        ? `${s.name} moved into the top ${s.winners} of ${s.campaign_title}`
        : `${s.name} dropped out of the top ${s.winners} of ${s.campaign_title}`,
    );
  }
  if (prev.checkedAt)
    for (const c of f.newCampaigns)
      if (c.opened_at > prev.checkedAt) lines.push(`new: ${c.title} (${c.prize})`);
  return { lines, state: { top }, feeAlertedUsd: fee.next };
}

/** "Since you last checked: a; b." or null. Pure. */
export const sinceLine = (lines: string[]) =>
  lines.length ? `Since you last checked: ${lines.join('; ')}.` : null;

/** The facts alerts compare, for this installation and every wallet it has used (v2.4 §5). */
export async function alertFacts(installationId: number): Promise<AlertFacts> {
  const db = sql();
  const mine = () => db`(p.installation_id = ${installationId}
    OR p.payout_wallet IN (SELECT wallet FROM installation_wallets WHERE installation_id = ${installationId}))`;
  const opened = await db<{ id: string; title: string; campaign: CampaignConfig; opened_at: Date }[]>`
    SELECT id, title, campaign, opened_at FROM programs
    WHERE status = 'active' AND campaign->>'kind' = 'earnkit' AND opened_at IS NOT NULL
      AND (ends_at IS NULL OR ends_at > now())`;
  const prizes = await db<{ id: number; name: string; prize_tx: string; prize_paid_at: Date; prize_amount: string | null; campaign: CampaignConfig }[]>`
    SELECT p.id, p.name, p.prize_tx, p.prize_paid_at, p.prize_amount, pr.campaign
    FROM projects p JOIN programs pr ON pr.id = p.program_id
    WHERE p.prize_paid_at IS NOT NULL AND p.prize_tx IS NOT NULL AND ${mine()}`;
  const open = await db<{ id: number; program_id: string; name: string; title: string; campaign: CampaignConfig }[]>`
    SELECT p.id, p.program_id, p.name, pr.title, pr.campaign
    FROM projects p JOIN programs pr ON pr.id = p.program_id
    WHERE pr.campaign->>'kind' = 'earnkit' AND pr.status = 'active' AND pr.ends_at > now()
      AND p.payout_wallet IS NOT NULL AND ${mine()}`;
  const all = await campaignCandidates([...new Set(open.map((e) => e.program_id))]);
  const tokens = await db<{ token_url: string; token_symbol: string | null; wallet: string | null }[]>`
    SELECT p.token_url, p.token_symbol, lower(COALESCE(p.creator_wallet, p.payout_wallet)) AS wallet
    FROM projects p WHERE p.token_url LIKE 'https://tokens.fun/coin/%' AND ${mine()} ORDER BY p.id DESC`;
  const wallets = tokens.map((t) => t.wallet).filter((w): w is string => !!w);
  const feesUsd = await cachedFeesUsd(wallets);
  return {
    newCampaigns: opened.map((c) => ({ id: c.id, title: c.title, prize: prizeColumn(c.campaign), opened_at: c.opened_at })),
    standings: open.map((e) => {
      const c = e.campaign as EarnKitCampaign;
      const entries = all.filter((x) => x.program_id === e.program_id);
      const self = entries.find((x) => x.id === e.id);
      return {
        project_id: e.id,
        name: e.name,
        campaign_title: e.title,
        winners: c.winners,
        scored: self?.score != null,
        inTop: pickWinners(entries, c.winners, c.min_score).winners.some((w) => w.id === e.id),
      };
    }),
    prizes: prizes.map((p) => ({
      project_id: p.id,
      name: p.name,
      won: wonUsdText(p.campaign, p.prize_amount),
      prize_tx: p.prize_tx,
      paid_at: p.prize_paid_at,
    })),
    fees: feesUsd === null || tokens.length === 0
      ? null
      : { usd: feesUsd, tokens: tokens.map((t) => ({ url: t.token_url, symbol: t.token_symbol })) },
  };
}

/**
 * The alerts for one reply, and the one-time daily-check note: reads the facts, then compares and
 * stores the new memory under the installation's row lock, so two replies at once show news once.
 */
export async function sinceYouLastChecked(installationId: number) {
  const facts = await alertFacts(installationId);
  const lines = await sql().begin(async (tx) => {
    const [row] = await tx<{ alerts_checked_at: Date | null; alert_state: AlertState | null; fee_alerted_usd: string | null }[]>`
      SELECT alerts_checked_at, alert_state, fee_alerted_usd FROM installations WHERE id = ${installationId} FOR UPDATE`;
    if (!row) return [];
    const out = alertLines(
      {
        checkedAt: row.alerts_checked_at,
        state: row.alert_state,
        feeAlertedUsd: row.fee_alerted_usd === null ? null : Number(row.fee_alerted_usd),
      },
      facts,
    );
    await tx`
      UPDATE installations SET alerts_checked_at = now(), alert_state = ${tx.json(out.state as never)},
        fee_alerted_usd = ${out.feeAlertedUsd}
      WHERE id = ${installationId}`;
    return out.lines;
  });
  const [offered] = await sql()<{ id: number }[]>`
    UPDATE installations SET schedule_offered_at = now()
    WHERE id = ${installationId} AND schedule_offered_at IS NULL RETURNING id`;
  return { lines, scheduleNote: offered ? SCHEDULE_NOTE : null };
}
```

```ts
/** Alerts never fail a tool: on any error the reply goes out without them. */
async function alertsFor(installationId: number) {
  try {
    return await sinceYouLastChecked(installationId);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[alerts]', err);
    return { lines: [] as string[], scheduleNote: null };
  }
}

/** v2.4 §4: a builder tool's reply opens with "Since you last checked" when there's news. */
async function withAlerts<T extends object>(installationId: number, result: T) {
  const a = await alertsFor(installationId);
  const line = sinceLine(a.lines);
  return {
    ...(line && { since_you_last_checked: line }),
    ...result,
    ...(a.scheduleNote && { schedule_daily_check: a.scheduleNote }),
  };
}
```

  - Wrap the returns of `get_program` (both returns), `prepare_submission`, `record_submission` and `launch_token` in `withAlerts(ctx.installationId, …)`. `sponsor_campaign` is not wrapped: it is the sponsor's tool, and it's dormant.
  - `list_programs`:

### Task 7: The cron and the automatic payout

Covers spec §3's automatic lifecycle and its safety rules (§8):
- **Scheduler and endpoint:** a GitHub Actions cron calls the endpoint. The endpoint is idempotent, secret-protected, timing-safe and rate-limited.
- **Scoring:** score while running, then freeze at the end, setting `score_final`.
- **Review:** a 48 h review before any payout.
- **Payout:** paid automatically from `CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY`, in rank order, with ties to the earlier entry and one prize per wallet and per installation.
  - Each payout is idempotent: `prize_tx` is written before the next one is sent.
  - Every winner is sanctions-screened.
  - It stops when the wallet is short, and the page says so.
  - It never pays anything but a frozen winner's payout wallet, with `amount_each` from the program file.
- **Fees:** the daily refresh of token fee balances (§4).

Create `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/http.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { bearerMatches, secretMatches } from './http';

const SECRET = 's'.repeat(40);

test('secretMatches: exact, constant time; an unset or short secret never matches', () => {
  assert.equal(secretMatches(SECRET, SECRET), true);
  assert.equal(secretMatches(SECRET + 'x', SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(secretMatches('', SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(secretMatches(undefined, SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(secretMatches('', ''), false);
  assert.equal(secretMatches('short', 'short'), false);
  assert.equal(secretMatches(SECRET, undefined), false);
});

test('bearerMatches reads "Bearer <secret>" only', () => {
  assert.equal(bearerMatches(`Bearer ${SECRET}`, SECRET), true);
  assert.equal(bearerMatches(SECRET, SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(bearerMatches(`Basic ${SECRET}`, SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(bearerMatches(null, SECRET), false);
});
```

Create `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/payout.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { keccak256, parseUnits, type Hex } from 'viem';
import { privateKeyToAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
import { payCampaign, payWinner, payoutAccount, type PayoutRpc, type PayoutStore } from './payout';
import type { EarnKitCampaign } from './campaigns';

const account = privateKeyToAccount('0x7c852118294e51e653712a81e05800f419141751be58f605c371e15141b007a6'); // anvil #3
const TOKEN = '0x534b7aAD1Cdb6F02eC48CAbe428f0D9131E40B07' as Hex;
const ONE = parseUnits('1', 18);

function fakeRpc(o: { chainId?: number; balance?: () => bigint; mined?: 'success' | 'reverted' | null } = {}) {
  const sent: Hex[] = [];
  const receipts = new Map<Hex, { status: 'success' | 'reverted' }>();
  const rpc: PayoutRpc = {
    getChainId: async () => o.chainId ?? 8453,
    nonce: async () => 7,
    fees: async () => ({ maxFeePerGas: 2_000_000_000n, maxPriorityFeePerGas: 1_000_000n }),
    estimateGas: async () => 50_000n,
    receipt: async (h) => receipts.get(h) ?? null,
    broadcast: async (raw) => {
      sent.push(raw);
      if (o.mined !== null) receipts.set(keccak256(raw), { status: o.mined ?? 'success' });
    },
    waitFor: async (h) => receipts.get(h) ?? null,
    balanceOf: async () => (o.balance ? o.balance() : 10n ** 30n),
  };
  return { rpc, sent, receipts };
}

function memStore(sentSoFar: () => number = () => 0) {
  const calls: unknown[][] = [];
  const store: PayoutStore = {
    sending: async (id, hash) => { calls.push(['sending', id, hash, sentSoFar()]); return true; },
    paid: async (id, amount) => { calls.push(['paid', id, amount]); },
    failed: async (id) => { calls.push(['failed', id]); },
    refused: async (id) => { calls.push(['refused', id]); },
  };
  return { store, calls };
}

const row = (id: number, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id,
  payout_wallet: `0x${String(id).padStart(40, '0')}`,
  payout_status: null as string | null,
  prize_tx: null as string | null,
  prize_raw_tx: null as string | null,
  ...extra,
});

test('payWinner: the signed transfer is stored before it is broadcast, then paid', async () => {
  const f = fakeRpc();
  const s = memStore(() => f.sent.length);
  const r = await payWinner(row(1), { token: TOKEN, amount: ONE, amountText: '1', account, rpc: f.rpc, store: s.store });
  assert.equal(r, 'paid');
  assert.equal(f.sent.length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls[0], ['sending', 1, keccak256(f.sent[0]), 0], 'stored while nothing was sent yet');
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls[1], ['paid', 1, '1']);
});

test('Review Focus 3: a transfer stored as sending is rebroadcast as the same bytes, never signed again', async () => {
  const signed = await account.signTransaction({
    chainId: 8453, type: 'eip1559', to: TOKEN, data: '0xa9059cbb', nonce: 7, gas: 60_000n,
    maxFeePerGas: 2_000_000_000n, maxPriorityFeePerGas: 1_000_000n,
  });
  const f = fakeRpc();
  const s = memStore();
  const r = await payWinner(
    row(1, { payout_status: 'sending', prize_tx: keccak256(signed), prize_raw_tx: signed }),
    { token: TOKEN, amount: ONE, amountText: '1', account, rpc: f.rpc, store: s.store },
  );
  assert.equal(r, 'paid');
  assert.deepEqual(f.sent, [signed]);
  assert.ok(!s.calls.some((c) => c[0] === 'sending'));
  // Already mined: no broadcast at all.
  const g = fakeRpc();
  g.receipts.set(keccak256(signed), { status: 'success' });
  await payWinner(row(1, { payout_status: 'sending', prize_tx: keccak256(signed), prize_raw_tx: signed }), {
    token: TOKEN, amount: ONE, amountText: '1', account, rpc: g.rpc, store: memStore().store,
  });
  assert.equal(g.sent.length, 0);
});

test('payWinner: a reverted transfer is cleared for a retry; an unmined one waits', async () => {
  const reverted = fakeRpc({ mined: 'reverted' });
  const s = memStore();
  assert.equal(await payWinner(row(1), { token: TOKEN, amount: ONE, amountText: '1', account, rpc: reverted.rpc, store: s.store }), 'failed');
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls.at(-1), ['failed', 1]);
  const stuck = fakeRpc({ mined: null });
  assert.equal(await payWinner(row(2), { token: TOKEN, amount: ONE, amountText: '1', account, rpc: stuck.rpc, store: memStore().store }), 'pending');
});

const campaign: EarnKitCampaign = {
  kind: 'earnkit',
  prize: { token: TOKEN, symbol: 'MINI', decimals: 18, chain: 'base' },
  winners: 2, amount_each: '1', prize_usd_total: 200, metric: 'unique_wallets', min_score: 10,
};
const entry = (id: number, score: number, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  ...row(id), score, submitted_at: new Date(Date.UTC(2026, 9, id)), installation_id: id, vetoed_at: null, ...extra,
});

test('payCampaign: rank order, a listed wallet skipped, stops when short, resumes once topped up', async () => {
  const W = '0x' + 'f'.repeat(40);
  const entries = [entry(1, 50, { payout_wallet: W }), entry(2, 40, { payout_wallet: W }), entry(3, 30), entry(4, 20)];
  let funds = ONE + ONE / 2n;
  const f = fakeRpc({ balance: () => funds - ONE * BigInt(f.sent.length) });
  const s = memStore();
  const listed = async (w: string) => w === entries[2].payout_wallet;
  const first = await payCampaign(campaign, entries, { account, rpc: f.rpc, store: s.store, listed });
  assert.equal(first.done, false);
  assert.match(first.note!, /holds 0\.5 MINI, less than the next prize \(1\)/);
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls.filter((c) => c[0] !== 'sending').map((c) => c.slice(0, 2)), [['paid', 1], ['refused', 3]]);
  funds += ONE;
  const second = await payCampaign(campaign, entries, { account, rpc: f.rpc, store: s.store, listed });
  assert.deepEqual(second, { done: true, note: null });
  assert.deepEqual(s.calls.filter((c) => c[0] === 'paid').map((c) => c[1]), [1, 4]);
  // Nothing more to pay: no transfer.
  const before = f.sent.length;
  assert.deepEqual(await payCampaign(campaign, entries, { account, rpc: f.rpc, store: s.store, listed }), { done: true, note: null });
  assert.equal(f.sent.length, before);
});

test('payCampaign never sends without the key or off Base mainnet', async () => {
  const f = fakeRpc({ chainId: 1 });
  const entries = [entry(1, 50)];
  assert.match((await payCampaign(campaign, entries, { account: null, rpc: f.rpc })).note!, /isn't set up yet/);
  assert.match((await payCampaign(campaign, entries, { account, rpc: f.rpc, store: memStore().store })).note!, /not Base mainnet/);
  assert.equal(f.sent.length, 0);
  assert.equal(payoutAccount({}), null);
  assert.throws(() => payoutAccount({ CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY: '0x1234' }), /CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY/);
});
```

Create `apps/frontend/lib/earnkit/cron.test.ts`:

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { cronPost, nextStep, scoreMaxAge } from './cron';
import { RateLimitError } from './rate-limit';

const ENDS = new Date('2026-10-18T23:59:00Z');
const at = (min: number) => new Date(ENDS.getTime() + min * 60_000);

test('nextStep: score, freeze, review, wait, pay, done', () => {
  const s = (state: 'running' | 'review' | 'paying' | 'paid', now: Date, allFinal = false, reviewUntil: Date | null = null) =>
    nextStep({ state, now, endsAt: ENDS, reviewUntil, allFinal });
  assert.equal(s('running', at(-60)), 'score');
  assert.equal(s('running', at(-60), true), 'score');
  assert.equal(s('running', at(14)), 'score', 'explorer grace');
  assert.equal(s('running', at(16)), 'freeze');
  assert.equal(s('running', at(16), true), 'review');
  assert.equal(s('review', at(100), true, at(2900)), 'wait');
  assert.equal(s('review', at(2900), true, at(2900)), 'pay');
  assert.equal(s('paying', at(3000), true), 'pay');
  assert.equal(s('paid', at(3000), true), 'done');
});

test('scoreMaxAge: 20 h, or 1 h in the last day', () => {
  assert.equal(scoreMaxAge(ENDS, at(-3 * 1440)), 20 * 3_600_000);
  assert.equal(scoreMaxAge(ENDS, at(-1439)), 3_600_000);
});

const SECRET = 'c'.repeat(40);
const req = (auth?: string) =>
  new Request('https://earnkit.com/api/cron', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'x-real-ip': '1.2.3.4', ...(auth && { authorization: auth }) } });

test('POST /api/cron: the secret is required (timing-safe), rate limited, and runs the jobs once', async () => {
  process.env.CRON_SECRET = SECRET;
  let runs = 0;
  const deps = { limit: async () => {}, run: async () => { runs++; return { ok: true }; } };
  assert.equal((await cronPost(req(), deps)).status, 401);
  assert.equal((await cronPost(req('Bearer nope'), deps)).status, 401);
  assert.equal(runs, 0);
  const ok = await cronPost(req(`Bearer ${SECRET}`), deps);
  assert.equal(ok.status, 200);
  assert.deepEqual(await ok.json(), { ok: true });
  assert.equal(runs, 1);
  const limited = await cronPost(req(`Bearer ${SECRET}`), { ...deps, limit: async () => { throw new RateLimitError('Too many cron calls.', 60); } });
  assert.equal(limited.status, 429);
  assert.equal(runs, 1);
  delete process.env.CRON_SECRET;
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `http.ts`.** Add `import { createHash, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';` and:

```ts
/**
 * Whether `given` equals `secret`, in constant time (both hashed first, so lengths don't leak). An
 * unset secret, or one under 32 characters, never matches (v2.4 §8). Pure; exported for tests.
 */
export function secretMatches(given: string | null | undefined, secret: string | undefined) {
  if (!secret || secret.length < 32 || !given) return false;
  return timingSafeEqual(
    createHash('sha256').update(given).digest(),
    createHash('sha256').update(secret).digest(),
  );
}

/** "Authorization: Bearer <secret>". Pure. */
export const bearerMatches = (header: string | null, secret: string | undefined) =>
  secretMatches(/^Bearer (\S+)$/.exec(header ?? '')?.[1], secret);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `payout.ts`.**

```ts
import {
  createPublicClient,
  encodeFunctionData,
  erc20Abi,
  formatUnits,
  http,
  keccak256,
  parseUnits,
  type Hex,
} from 'viem';
import { privateKeyToAccount, type LocalAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
import { base } from 'viem/chains';
import type { EarnKitCampaign } from './campaigns';
import { privateKeyProblem } from './cli';
import { sql } from './db';
import { BASE_MAINNET, sanctionedWallets } from './sanctions';
import { pickWinners, type Candidate } from './winners';

/**
 * The campaign payout wallet (v2.4 §3, §8): CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY, the only key on Railway,
 * holding one campaign's prize plus gas. Null when unset; a malformed key throws.
 */
export function payoutAccount(env: Record<string, string | undefined> = process.env): LocalAccount | null {
  const key = env.CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY?.trim();
  if (!key) return null;
  const problem = privateKeyProblem('CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY', key);
  if (problem) throw new Error(problem);
  return privateKeyToAccount(key as Hex);
}

type Receipt = { status: 'success' | 'reverted' };
/** The chain reads and the one write a payout needs; replaced in tests. */
export type PayoutRpc = {
  getChainId: () => Promise<number>;
  nonce: (address: Hex) => Promise<number>;
  fees: () => Promise<{ maxFeePerGas: bigint; maxPriorityFeePerGas: bigint }>;
  estimateGas: (a: { account: Hex; to: Hex; data: Hex }) => Promise<bigint>;
  receipt: (hash: Hex) => Promise<Receipt | null>;
  broadcast: (raw: Hex) => Promise<void>;
  waitFor: (hash: Hex, timeoutMs: number) => Promise<Receipt | null>;
  balanceOf: (token: Hex, holder: Hex) => Promise<bigint>;
};

/** Base over BASE_RPC_URL (the public RPC by default). */
export function payoutRpc(url = process.env.BASE_RPC_URL): PayoutRpc {
  const c = createPublicClient({ chain: base, transport: http(url) });
  return {
    getChainId: () => c.getChainId(),
    nonce: (address) => c.getTransactionCount({ address, blockTag: 'pending' }),
    fees: async () => {
      const f = await c.estimateFeesPerGas();
      return { maxFeePerGas: f.maxFeePerGas!, maxPriorityFeePerGas: f.maxPriorityFeePerGas! };
    },
    estimateGas: (a) => c.estimateGas(a),
    receipt: (hash) => c.getTransactionReceipt({ hash }).then((r) => ({ status: r.status }), () => null),
    broadcast: async (raw) => {
      await c.sendRawTransaction({ serializedTransaction: raw }).catch((err: unknown) => {
        // Rebroadcasting a known transaction is fine: it can only be mined once.
        if (!/already known|known transaction|nonce too low/i.test(String(err))) throw err;
      });
    },
    waitFor: (hash, timeout) =>
      c.waitForTransactionReceipt({ hash, timeout }).then((r) => ({ status: r.status }), () => null),
    balanceOf: (token, holder) =>
      c.readContract({ address: token, abi: erc20Abi, functionName: 'balanceOf', args: [holder] }),
  };
}

/** Payout writes; each is one statement on the winner's row. */
export type PayoutStore = {
  /** Stores the signed transfer before it is sent; false when another run already did. */
  sending: (id: number, hash: Hex, raw: Hex) => Promise<boolean>;
  paid: (id: number, amount: string) => Promise<void>;
  /** A reverted transfer: cleared so the next run signs a new one. */
  failed: (id: number) => Promise<void>;
  /** The payout wallet is on the sanctions list: the next entry takes the slot. */
  refused: (id: number) => Promise<void>;
};

export const dbPayoutStore: PayoutStore = {
  sending: async (id, hash, raw) =>
    (
      await sql()`
        UPDATE projects SET payout_status = 'sending', prize_tx = ${hash}, prize_raw_tx = ${raw}
        WHERE id = ${id} AND prize_tx IS NULL RETURNING id`
    ).length > 0,
  paid: async (id, amount) => {
    await sql()`
      UPDATE projects SET payout_status = 'paid', prize_paid_at = now(), prize_amount = ${amount}, prize_raw_tx = NULL
      WHERE id = ${id}`;
  },
  failed: async (id) => {
    await sql()`
      UPDATE projects SET payout_status = NULL, prize_tx = NULL, prize_raw_tx = NULL
      WHERE id = ${id} AND payout_status = 'sending'`;
  },
  refused: async (id) => {
    await sql()`UPDATE projects SET payout_status = 'refused' WHERE id = ${id} AND prize_tx IS NULL`;
  },
};

export type PayoutRow = Pick<Candidate, 'id' | 'payout_wallet' | 'payout_status' | 'prize_tx'> & {
  prize_raw_tx: string | null;
};

/**
 * Pays one winner, idempotently (v2.4 §3; Review Focus 3): the ERC-20 transfer is signed, and its
 * hash and raw bytes stored ('sending') before it is broadcast. A crash, or another run, finds
 * them and rebroadcasts the same bytes (one nonce: mined at most once) instead of signing again.
 * 'paid' once mined; 'pending' while not; 'failed' (cleared for a retry) if it reverted.
 */
export async function payWinner(
  row: PayoutRow,
  a: { token: Hex; amount: bigint; amountText: string; account: LocalAccount; rpc: PayoutRpc; store: PayoutStore },
): Promise<'paid' | 'pending' | 'failed'> {
  if (row.payout_status === 'paid') return 'paid';
  let raw = row.prize_raw_tx as Hex | null;
  let hash = row.prize_tx as Hex | null;
  if (!raw || !hash) {
    const data = encodeFunctionData({
      abi: erc20Abi,
      functionName: 'transfer',
      args: [row.payout_wallet as Hex, a.amount],
    });
    const [chainId, nonce, fees, gas] = await Promise.all([
      a.rpc.getChainId(),
      a.rpc.nonce(a.account.address),
      a.rpc.fees(),
      a.rpc.estimateGas({ account: a.account.address, to: a.token, data }),
    ]);
    raw = await a.account.signTransaction({
      chainId,
      type: 'eip1559',
      to: a.token,
      data,
      nonce,
      gas: (gas * 12n) / 10n,
      maxFeePerGas: fees.maxFeePerGas,
      maxPriorityFeePerGas: fees.maxPriorityFeePerGas,
    });
    hash = keccak256(raw);
    if (!(await a.store.sending(row.id, hash, raw))) return 'pending';
    Object.assign(row, { prize_tx: hash, prize_raw_tx: raw, payout_status: 'sending' });
  }
  let receipt = await a.rpc.receipt(hash);
  if (!receipt) {
    await a.rpc.broadcast(raw);
    receipt = await a.rpc.waitFor(hash, 60_000);
  }
  if (!receipt) return 'pending';
  if (receipt.status === 'success') {
    await a.store.paid(row.id, a.amountText);
    row.payout_status = 'paid';
    return 'paid';
  }
  await a.store.failed(row.id);
  Object.assign(row, { prize_tx: null, prize_raw_tx: null, payout_status: null });
  return 'failed';
}

export type PayDeps = {
  account?: LocalAccount | null;
  rpc?: PayoutRpc;
  store?: PayoutStore;
  /** Whether a wallet is on the sanctions list (production fails closed: it throws). */
  listed?: (wallet: string) => Promise<boolean>;
};

/**
 * Pays an EarnKit campaign's frozen winners (v2.4 §3, §8), in rank order: only winners from
 * pickWinners (one prize per payout wallet and per installation, vetoes applied), amount_each from
 * the program file, to their payout wallet only, each screened first (a listed wallet is skipped
 * and the next entry moves up). Stops with a note when the wallet holds less than the next prize,
 * or a transfer isn't mined yet; the next run continues. Never sends off Base mainnet.
 */
export async function payCampaign(
  c: EarnKitCampaign,
  entries: (Candidate & { prize_raw_tx: string | null })[],
  deps: PayDeps = {},
): Promise<{ done: boolean; note: string | null }> {
  const account = deps.account === undefined ? payoutAccount() : deps.account;
  if (!account)
    return { done: false, note: "The payout wallet isn't set up yet (CAMPAIGN_PAYOUT_PRIVATE_KEY); nothing was sent." };
  const rpc = deps.rpc ?? payoutRpc();
  const store = deps.store ?? dbPayoutStore;
  const listed =
    deps.listed ?? (async (w: string) => (await sanctionedWallets([w], BASE_MAINNET)).length > 0);
  if ((await rpc.getChainId()) !== BASE_MAINNET)
    return { done: false, note: 'BASE_RPC_URL is not Base mainnet; nothing was sent.' };
  const token = c.prize.token as Hex;
  const amount = parseUnits(c.amount_each, c.prize.decimals);
  for (let step = 0; step <= entries.length + c.winners; step++) {
    const { winners } = pickWinners(entries, c.winners, c.min_score);
    const next = winners.find((w) => w.payout_status !== 'paid');
    if (!next)
      return { done: true, note: winners.length ? null : 'No entry reached the minimum score; nothing to pay.' };
    if (!next.prize_tx) {
      if (await listed(next.payout_wallet)) {
        await store.refused(next.id);
        next.payout_status = 'refused';
        continue;
      }
      const balance = await rpc.balanceOf(token, account.address);
      if (balance < amount)
        return {
          done: false,
          note: `The payout wallet ${account.address} holds ${formatUnits(balance, c.prize.decimals)} ${c.prize.symbol}, less than the next prize (${c.amount_each}). Payouts resume once it is topped up.`,
        };
    }
    const r = await payWinner(next, { token, amount, amountText: c.amount_each, account, rpc, store });
    if (r === 'paid') continue;
    return {
      done: false,
      note: r === 'failed'
        ? `The transfer to entry #${next.id} failed; it is retried on the next run.`
        : `Waiting for the transfer to entry #${next.id} to be mined.`,
    };
  }
  return { done: false, note: 'Payout stopped early; it continues on the next run.' };
}

/** Proof of funds for the campaign page (v2.4 §3): the payout wallet and its live prize balance. */
export async function payoutFunds(c: EarnKitCampaign) {
  try {
    const account = payoutAccount();
    if (!account) return null;
    const balance = await payoutRpc().balanceOf(c.prize.token as Hex, account.address);
    return { wallet: account.address, balance: formatUnits(balance, c.prize.decimals) };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[funds]', err);
    return null;
  }
}
```

Check the short-wallet test: 1.5 MINI funds; entry 1 paid (`sent` is now 1, so the balance reads 0.5); entry 2 is skipped (same wallet as 1); entry 3 is listed, so it is refused; entry 4: 0.5 < 1, so the run stops with the note.

- [ ] **Step 5: `cron.ts`.**

```ts
import { sql } from './db';
import { FINAL_GRACE_MS, REVIEW_MS, type CampaignConfig, type EarnKitCampaign } from './campaigns';
import { scoreCampaignEntries, type ScoreDeps, type ScoreRow } from './campaign-score';
import { launchedCreatorWallets, refreshFees } from './fees';
import { bearerMatches, json } from './http';
import { payCampaign, type PayDeps } from './payout';
import { RateLimitError, clientIp, enforce } from './rate-limit';
import { withScoringLock } from './sponsor';
import type { Candidate, RunState } from './winners';

/** Work per cron call; the hourly run picks up the rest. */
export const CRON_BUDGET_MS = 4 * 60_000;
/** An entry still unscorable this long after the end is frozen with its last score (its error shown in the review). */
export const FREEZE_GIVE_UP_MS = 24 * 3_600_000;

/** How old a running score may get: 20 h, or 1 h in the campaign's last day (v2.4 §3 Scheduler). Pure. */
export const scoreMaxAge = (endsAt: Date, now: Date) =>
  endsAt.getTime() - now.getTime() <= 86_400_000 ? 3_600_000 : 20 * 3_600_000;

export type Step = 'score' | 'freeze' | 'review' | 'wait' | 'pay' | 'done';

/** What the cron does for one EarnKit campaign now (v2.4 §3): its state machine. Pure; exported for tests. */
export function nextStep(a: { state: RunState; now: Date; endsAt: Date; reviewUntil: Date | null; allFinal: boolean }): Step {
  if (a.state === 'paid') return 'done';
  if (a.state === 'paying') return 'pay';
  if (a.state === 'review') return a.reviewUntil && a.now >= a.reviewUntil ? 'pay' : 'wait';
  if (a.now.getTime() < a.endsAt.getTime() + FINAL_GRACE_MS) return 'score';
  return a.allFinal ? 'review' : 'freeze';
}

type Program = {
  id: string;
  starts_at: Date;
  ends_at: Date;
  campaign: CampaignConfig;
  state: RunState | null;
  review_until: Date | null;
};
type Entry = ScoreRow & Candidate & { prize_raw_tx: string | null };

const entriesOf = (programId: string) => sql()<Entry[]>`
  SELECT id, contracts, payout_wallet, deployers, score, scored_at, scored_through, score_final,
    submitted_at, installation_id, vetoed_at, payout_status, prize_tx, prize_raw_tx
  FROM projects
  WHERE program_id = ${programId} AND payout_wallet IS NOT NULL AND contracts IS NOT NULL
  ORDER BY id`;

/** Injection for the e2e (scoring reads, payout chain and wallet, the fee source). */
export type CronDeps = { score?: ScoreDeps; pay?: PayDeps; refreshFees?: (wallets: string[]) => Promise<number> };

/** One campaign, one step. The entries are read again after the review closes, so a last-minute veto counts. */
async function advance(p: Program, now: Date, deadline: number, deps: CronDeps) {
  await sql()`INSERT INTO campaign_runs (program_id) VALUES (${p.id}) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`;
  const entries = await entriesOf(p.id);
  const step = nextStep({
    state: p.state ?? 'running',
    now,
    endsAt: p.ends_at,
    reviewUntil: p.review_until,
    allFinal: entries.every((e) => e.score_final),
  });
  if (step === 'score') {
    const out = await scoreCampaignEntries(p, entries, { now, deadline, final: false, maxAgeMs: scoreMaxAge(p.ends_at, now) }, deps.score);
    return { step, scored: out.filter((o) => o.result === 'scored').length, failed: out.filter((o) => o.result === 'failed').length };
  }
  if (step === 'freeze') {
    const out = await scoreCampaignEntries(p, entries, { now, deadline, final: true, maxAgeMs: 0 }, deps.score);
    if (now.getTime() >= p.ends_at.getTime() + FREEZE_GIVE_UP_MS)
      await sql()`
        UPDATE projects SET score_final = true
        WHERE program_id = ${p.id} AND payout_wallet IS NOT NULL AND NOT score_final`;
    return { step, final: out.filter((o) => o.result === 'final').length, failed: out.filter((o) => o.result === 'failed').length };
  }
  if (step === 'review') {
    await sql()`
      UPDATE campaign_runs SET state = 'review', frozen_at = ${now},
        review_until = ${new Date(now.getTime() + REVIEW_MS)}, updated_at = now()
      WHERE program_id = ${p.id} AND state = 'running'`;
    return { step };
  }
  if (step === 'pay') {
    await sql()`UPDATE campaign_runs SET state = 'paying', updated_at = now() WHERE program_id = ${p.id} AND state = 'review'`;
    const fresh = (await entriesOf(p.id)).filter((e) => e.score_final);
    const r = await payCampaign(p.campaign as EarnKitCampaign, fresh, deps.pay);
    await sql()`
      UPDATE campaign_runs SET state = ${r.done ? 'paid' : 'paying'}, note = ${r.note}, updated_at = now()
      WHERE program_id = ${p.id}`;
    return { step, done: r.done, note: r.note };
  }
  return { step };
}

/**
 * The daily jobs (v2.4 §3, §4), idempotent: for every EarnKit campaign that is live or not yet
 * paid out, one lifecycle step under the campaign's lease (a second concurrent call gets 'busy');
 * one campaign's failure never stops the others; then the token-fee refresh for every creator
 * wallet. `now` is injectable for the e2e (production: the real time).
 */
export async function runCron(opts: { now?: Date; budgetMs?: number; deps?: CronDeps } = {}) {
  const now = opts.now ?? new Date();
  const deadline = Date.now() + (opts.budgetMs ?? CRON_BUDGET_MS);
  const deps = opts.deps ?? {};
  const programs = await sql()<Program[]>`
    SELECT p.id, p.starts_at, p.ends_at, p.campaign, r.state, r.review_until
    FROM programs p LEFT JOIN campaign_runs r ON r.program_id = p.id
    WHERE p.campaign->>'kind' = 'earnkit' AND p.starts_at IS NOT NULL AND p.ends_at IS NOT NULL
      AND p.status = 'active' AND COALESCE(r.state, 'running') <> 'paid'
    ORDER BY p.ends_at`;
  const campaigns: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  for (const p of programs) {
    try {
      const r = await withScoringLock(p.id, () => advance(p, now, deadline, deps), (opts.budgetMs ?? CRON_BUDGET_MS) + 60_000);
      campaigns.push({ id: p.id, ...(r === 'busy' ? { step: 'busy' } : r) });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[cron]', p.id, err);
      campaigns.push({ id: p.id, step: 'error', note: String(err).slice(0, 200) });
    }
  }
  let fees = 0;
  try {
    fees = await (deps.refreshFees ?? refreshFees)(await launchedCreatorWallets());
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[cron] fees', err);
  }
  return { now: now.toISOString(), campaigns, fee_wallets: fees };
}

/**
 * POST /api/cron (app/api/cron/route.ts re-exports it): rate limited per IP, then Bearer
 * CRON_SECRET compared in constant time, then the jobs. `deps` for tests.
 */
export async function cronPost(
  request: Request,
  deps: { limit?: (ip: string) => Promise<unknown>; run?: () => Promise<unknown> } = {},
) {
  try {
    await (deps.limit ?? ((ip: string) => enforce('cron', ip, 'Too many cron calls from this address.')))(
      clientIp(request.headers),
    );
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError)
      return json({ error: err.message }, 429, { 'Retry-After': String(err.retryAfter) });
    throw err;
  }
  if (!bearerMatches(request.headers.get('authorization'), process.env.CRON_SECRET))
    return json({ error: 'unauthorized' }, 401);
  return json(await (deps.run ?? (() => runCron()))());
}
```

`campaigns` only lists `status = 'active'` programs. An ended campaign stays `active` in its file, so it keeps running through review and payout; Campaign #1 (closed) is skipped until golive. `advance` passes `p` (it has `starts_at` and `ends_at`) to `scoreCampaignEntries`.

```ts
// The daily jobs (v2.4 §3): .github/workflows/earnkit-cron.yml calls this with CRON_SECRET.
export { cronPost as POST } from '@/lib/earnkit/cron';
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
```

`.github/workflows/earnkit-cron.yml`:

```yaml
# EarnKit's automatic campaign lifecycle (v2.4 §3): scoring, freezing, the 48 h review, payouts,
# and the token-fee refresh. Runs hourly; the endpoint scores a running campaign only once its
# scores are 20 h old (1 h in the last day), so the effect is daily plus hourly at the end.
# Free tier: one short job an hour. Needs the repo secret CRON_SECRET (same value as on Railway).
name: EarnKit cron
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '15 * * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:
permissions: {}
concurrency:
  group: earnkit-cron
  cancel-in-progress: false
jobs:
  cron:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - name: POST https://earnkit.com/api/cron
        env:
          CRON_SECRET: ${{ secrets.CRON_SECRET }}
        run: |
          test -n "$CRON_SECRET" || { echo "CRON_SECRET is not set"; exit 1; }
          curl -fsS --max-time 540 -X POST \
            -H "Authorization: Bearer $CRON_SECRET" \
            https://earnkit.com/api/cron
```

```ts
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { VetoError, vetoKey, vetoKeyMatches, vetoLink, vetoPost } from './veto';

const SECRET = 'v'.repeat(48);
const KEY = vetoKey('c1', SECRET)!;

test('vetoKey: per campaign, from ADMIN_VETO_SECRET; none without a long enough secret', () => {
  assert.match(KEY, /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
  assert.notEqual(vetoKey('c2', SECRET), KEY);
  assert.equal(vetoKey('c1', 'short'), null);
  assert.equal(vetoKey('c1', undefined), null);
  assert.equal(vetoKeyMatches('c1', KEY, SECRET), true);
  assert.equal(vetoKeyMatches('c2', KEY, SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(vetoKeyMatches('c1', '', SECRET), false);
  assert.equal(vetoLink('https://earnkit.com', 'c1', SECRET), `https://earnkit.com/admin/veto/c1?key=${KEY}`);
});

const post = (fields: Record<string, string>) =>
  new Request('https://earnkit.com/api/admin/veto', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded', 'x-real-ip': '1.2.3.4' },
    body: new URLSearchParams(fields).toString(),
  });

test('POST /api/admin/veto: the key, an entry id, then the veto; a closed review is a 409', async () => {
  process.env.ADMIN_VETO_SECRET = SECRET;
  const calls: unknown[][] = [];
  const deps = { limit: async () => {}, veto: async (...a: unknown[]) => { calls.push(a); } };
  assert.equal((await vetoPost(post({ slug: 'c1', key: 'nope', entry: '7' }), deps)).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await vetoPost(post({ slug: 'c1', key: KEY, entry: 'x' }), deps)).status, 400);
  assert.equal(calls.length, 0);
  const ok = await vetoPost(post({ slug: 'c1', key: KEY, entry: '7', reason: 'wash trading' }), deps);
  assert.equal(ok.status, 303);
  assert.equal(ok.headers.get('location'), `/admin/veto/c1?key=${KEY}&removed=7`);
  assert.deepEqual(calls, [['c1', 7, 'wash trading']]);
  const closed = await vetoPost(post({ slug: 'c1', key: KEY, entry: '7' }), {
    ...deps,
    veto: async () => { throw new VetoError('closed'); },
  });
  assert.equal(closed.status, 409);
  delete process.env.ADMIN_VETO_SECRET;
});

test('Review Focus 4: opening the link (GET) never removes anything', () => {
  const app = join(import.meta.dirname, '../../app');
  const route = readFileSync(join(app, 'api/admin/veto/route.ts'), 'utf8');

  assert.doesNotMatch(route, /\bGET\b/);

  assert.match(page, /method="post"/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(page, /vetoEntry/);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `veto.ts`.**

```ts
import { createHmac } from 'node:crypto';
import { sql } from './db';
import { json, readParams, secretMatches } from './http';
import { RateLimitError, clientIp, enforce } from './rate-limit';
import { plainText } from './untrusted-text';

export class VetoError extends Error {}

/** The secret per-campaign veto key (v2.4 §3): HMAC-SHA256 of the slug under ADMIN_VETO_SECRET (32+ characters), or null. Pure. */
export function vetoKey(slug: string, secret = process.env.ADMIN_VETO_SECRET) {
  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) return null;
  return createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`veto:${slug}`).digest('base64url');
}

/** Whether `given` is the campaign's veto key, in constant time. Pure; exported for tests. */
export const vetoKeyMatches = (slug: string, given: string | null | undefined, secret = process.env.ADMIN_VETO_SECRET) => {
  const key = vetoKey(slug, secret);
  return key !== null && secretMatches(given, key);
};

/** The owner's review link (pnpm campaign veto-link prints it); never sent anywhere by EarnKit. */
export const vetoLink = (origin: string, slug: string, secret = process.env.ADMIN_VETO_SECRET) => {
  const key = vetoKey(slug, secret);
  return key ? `${origin.replace(/\/$/, '')}/admin/veto/${slug}?key=${key}` : null;
};

/**
 * Removes an entry during its campaign's 48 h review (v2.4 §3), in one statement so it can't land
 * after the payout started; the next eligible entry moves up (pickWinners). The reason is public.
 */
export async function vetoEntry(slug: string, entryId: number, reason: string) {
  const why = plainText(reason, true).slice(0, 200) || 'Removed in review';
  const rows = await sql()`
    UPDATE projects SET vetoed_at = now(), veto_reason = ${why}
    WHERE id = ${entryId} AND program_id = ${slug} AND prize_tx IS NULL AND vetoed_at IS NULL
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM campaign_runs r WHERE r.program_id = ${slug} AND r.state = 'review')
    RETURNING id`;
  if (rows.length === 0)
    throw new VetoError(`Entry ${entryId} can't be removed: it isn't an unpaid entry of ${slug} during its review.`);
}

/**
 * POST /api/admin/veto (form: slug, key, entry, reason), re-exported by the route. GET doesn't
 * exist: link previews and scanners can't remove anything (Review Focus 4). Rate limited per IP;
 * a wrong key is a plain 404.
 */
export async function vetoPost(
  request: Request,
  deps: { limit?: (ip: string) => Promise<unknown>; veto?: (slug: string, id: number, reason: string) => Promise<void> } = {},
) {
  try {
    await (deps.limit ?? ((ip: string) => enforce('veto', ip, 'Too many veto requests from this address.')))(
      clientIp(request.headers),
    );
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError)
      return json({ error: err.message }, 429, { 'Retry-After': String(err.retryAfter) });
    throw err;
  }
  const form = await readParams(request);
  const slug = form.get('slug') ?? '';
  const key = form.get('key') ?? '';
  if (!vetoKeyMatches(slug, key)) return new Response('Not found', { status: 404 });
  const entry = Number(form.get('entry'));
  if (!Number.isInteger(entry) || entry <= 0) return json({ error: 'entry: give the entry id' }, 400);
  try {
    await (deps.veto ?? vetoEntry)(slug, entry, form.get('reason') ?? '');
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof VetoError) return json({ error: err.message }, 409);
    throw err;
  }
  return new Response(null, {
    status: 303,
    headers: { Location: `/admin/veto/${encodeURIComponent(slug)}?key=${encodeURIComponent(key)}&removed=${entry}` },
  });
}
```

`apps/frontend/app/api/admin/veto/route.ts`:

- [ ] **Step 4: Review data.** In `leaderboard.ts` (imports: `pickWinners`, `rankEntries`, `standingText`, `type Candidate`, `type RunState` from `./winners`; `payoutFunds` from `./payout`; `type EarnKitCampaign` from `./campaigns`):

```ts
/** One entry with its review evidence (v2.4 §3): counted users, smart accounts, users shared with other entries, Builder Code share. */
export type ReviewRow = Candidate & {
  name: string;
  rank: number;
  contracts: string[];
  deployers: string[] | null;
  live_url: string | null;
  repo_url: string | null;
  code_share: number | null;
  score_error: string | null;
  score_partial: boolean;
  veto_reason: string | null;
  counted: number;
  smart: number;
  shared: number;
};

/** A campaign's entries ranked with evidence, and the winners as the cron would pay them now. */
export async function reviewRows(slug: string, c: EarnKitCampaign) {
  const rows = await sql()<Omit<ReviewRow, 'rank'>[]>`
    SELECT p.id, p.name, p.score, p.submitted_at, p.payout_wallet, p.installation_id, p.vetoed_at,
      p.veto_reason, p.payout_status, p.prize_tx, p.live_url, p.repo_url, p.contracts, p.deployers,
      p.code_share, p.score_error, p.score_partial,
      (SELECT count(*) FROM entry_wallets w WHERE w.project_id = p.id)::int AS counted,
      (SELECT count(*) FROM entry_wallets w WHERE w.project_id = p.id AND w.kind = 'smart')::int AS smart,
      (SELECT count(*) FROM entry_wallets w WHERE w.project_id = p.id AND EXISTS (
         SELECT 1 FROM entry_wallets o JOIN projects op ON op.id = o.project_id
         WHERE o.wallet = w.wallet AND o.project_id <> p.id AND op.program_id = p.program_id))::int AS shared
    FROM projects p WHERE p.program_id = ${slug} AND p.payout_wallet IS NOT NULL`;
  const ranked = rankEntries(rows).map((r, i) => ({
    ...r,
    rank: i + 1,
    live_url: shownUrl(r.live_url),
    repo_url: shownUrl(r.repo_url),
  }));
  const { winners, skipped } = pickWinners(ranked, c.winners, c.min_score);
  return { ranked, picks: winners, skipped };
}
```
